\eqref{ifff}\iff\delta(2\delta-1)(3\delta-2)>(\delta^{2}-5\delta+3)^{2}\impliedby 7-2\sqrt{10}<\delta<\frac{5-\sqrt{13}}{2}.

Hence, based on the above, we see that

\tilde{p}_{2}(\delta)>3
for any
\delta\in(7-2\sqrt{10},1)
.
∎

Next, we concern the
L^{p}(I)
-estimate for
(r^{m}\rho)^{\frac{1}{p}}u
.

Lemma 4.7
.

Let
\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta)
be defined as in
Lemma
4.5
. Then, for any
p\in[2,\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta))
and
\varepsilon\in(0,1)
, there exist
c_{p}>0
, depending only on
(n,\delta,p)
, and
C(p,\varepsilon)>0
such that

(4.17)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|(r^{m}\rho)^{\frac{1}{p}}u\big|_{p}^{p}+a_{1}c_{p}\big|(r^{m}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}|u|^{\frac{p-2}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u\big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(p,\varepsilon)\big(1+\big|(r^{m}\rho)^{\frac{1}{p}}u\big|_{p}^{p}\big)+\varepsilon\big|(r^{m}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1})^{\frac{1}{p}}v\big|_{p}^{p}.

Proof.

We divide the proof into four steps.

1.
Let
\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta)
be defined as in Lemma
4.5
. Multiplying
\eqref{e1.5}_{2}
by
r^{m}|u|^{p-2}u
with
p\in[2,\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta))
gives

(4.18)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p}(r^{m}\rho|u|^{p})_{t}+2a_{1}r^{m}\rho^{\delta}|u|^{p-2}\Big(\underline{\delta(p-1)u_{r}^{2}-mp(1-\delta)u_{r}\frac{u}{r}+(\delta m^{2}-m^{2}+m)\frac{u^{2}}{r^{2}}}_{:=I_{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle=\Big(r^{m}\big(\underline{2a_{1}\rho^{\delta}|u|^{p-2}u\big(\delta u_{r}+(\delta-1)m\frac{u}{r}\big)-A\rho^{\gamma}|u|^{p-2}u-\frac{1}{p}\rho u|u|^{p}\big)}_{\mathcal{B}_{2}}\Big)_{r}

\displaystyle+Ar^{m}\rho^{\gamma}|u|^{p-2}\big((p-1)u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big).

2.
We show that, for any
p\in[2,\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta))
, there exists a constant
c_{p}>0
depending only on
(p,\delta,n)
such that

(4.19)

I_{2}\geq c_{p}\big(u_{r}^{2}+\frac{u^{2}}{r^{2}}\big).

First, note that
I_{2}
can be written in the following simple form:

I_{2}=\delta(p-1)X^{2}-mp(1-\delta)XY+(\delta m^{2}-m^{2}+m)Y^{2},

where
(X,Y)=(u_{r},\frac{u}{r})
, and its discriminant
\mathscr{D}(p;\delta)
is

\mathscr{D}(p;\delta)=m^{2}(1-\delta)^{2}p^{2}-4\delta(\delta m^{2}-m^{2}+m)p+4\delta(\delta m^{2}-m^{2}+m),

which takes the same form as the quadratic equation given in Lemma
4.6
.

Consequently, by Lemma
4.6
,
\mathscr{D}(\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta);\delta)=0
, and
\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta)>n
is the larger root of the equation
\mathscr{D}(p;\delta)=0
. Moreover, since
\mathscr{D}(2;\delta)<0
(see the proof of Lemma
4.2
),
we obtain that
\mathscr{D}(p;\delta)<0
for any
p\in[2,\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta))
, which implies claim (
4.19
).

3.
We prove
r^{m}\mathrm{D}_{r}\mathcal{B}_{2}\in L^{1}(I)
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
. Then Lemma
A.3
leads to

(4.20)

\int_{0}^{\infty}(r^{m}\mathcal{B}_{2})_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r=0.

Indeed, since
|\mathrm{D}_{r}\mathcal{B}_{2}|\leq C(p)|u|^{p-2}|\mathrm{D}_{r}\mathcal{B}_{1}|
and
u\in C(\bar{I})
for
t\in(0,T]
due to (
4.8
), we can obtain claim (
4.20
) by following an argument similar to the proof of (
4.12
).

4.
Integrating (
4.18
) over
I
, together with (
4.19
)–(
4.20
), yields

(4.21)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|(r^{m}\rho)^{\frac{1}{p}}u\big|_{p}^{p}+2a_{1}c_{p}\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\rho^{\delta}|u|^{p-2}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq A\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\rho^{\gamma}|u|^{p-2}\big((p-1)u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\,\mathrm{d}r:=I_{3}.

To estimate
I_{3}
, we see from Lemmas
4.3
–
4.4
and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(4.22)

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\leq C(p)\big|(r^{m}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}|u|^{\frac{p-2}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u\big|_{2}\big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\rho^{\gamma-\frac{\delta}{2}}|u|^{\frac{p-2}{2}}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a_{1}c_{p}}{32}\big|(r^{m}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}|u|^{\frac{p-2}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u\big|_{2}^{2}+C(p)\big(\big|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\rho^{\gamma-\frac{\delta}{2}}|u|^{\frac{p-2}{2}}\big|_{2}^{2}+\big|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\rho^{\gamma-\frac{\delta}{2}}|u|^{\frac{p-2}{2}}\big|_{2}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a_{1}c_{p}}{32}\big|(r^{m}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}|u|^{\frac{p-2}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u\big|_{2}^{2}+C(p)\big|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{p+m-2}{p\gamma-p\delta+\delta}}\rho\big|_{p\gamma-p\delta+\delta}^{\frac{2p\gamma-2p\delta+2\delta}{p}}\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{p}}u\big|_{p}^{p-2}

\displaystyle+C(p)|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{m(1+\delta-2\gamma)}|_{\infty}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{m}\rho|_{\frac{2p\gamma-p(1+\delta)+2}{2}}^{\frac{2p\gamma-p(1+\delta)+2}{p}}\big|(r^{m}\rho)^{\frac{1}{p}}u\big|_{p}^{p-2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a_{1}c_{p}}{16}\big|(r^{m}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}|u|^{\frac{p-2}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u\big|_{2}^{2}+C(p)\Big(1+\big|(r^{m}\rho)^{\frac{1}{p}}u\big|_{p}^{p}+\underline{\big|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{p+m-2}{p\gamma-p\delta+\delta}}\rho\big|_{p\gamma-p\delta+\delta}^{p\gamma-p\delta+\delta}}_{:=I_{3,1}}\Big),

where we have used Lemma
4.3
with the fact that
\frac{2p\gamma-p(1+\delta)+2}{2}>1
.

4.1. Estimate for
I_{3,1}
when
n=2
.
Note that, if
n=2
(
m=1
),

\frac{p+m-2}{p\gamma-p\delta+\delta}=\frac{p-1}{p\gamma-p\delta+\delta}>0.

Thus, we obtain from Lemma
4.4
that

(4.23)

I_{3,1}\leq C(p).

4.2. Estimate for
I_{3,1}
when
n=3
.
Set

\mathfrak{a}_{1}:=p,\qquad\mathfrak{b}_{1}:=p\gamma-p\delta+\delta,\qquad\ \mathcal{I}_{(l_{1},l_{2})}:=\int_{0}^{1}r^{l_{1}}\rho^{l_{2}}\,\mathrm{d}r.

First, it follows from (
4.9
), Lemma
4.3
(with
\omega=\frac{1}{2}
), integration by parts, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that, for any
\varepsilon_{1}\in(0,1)
,

(4.24)

\displaystyle I_{3,1}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{I}_{(\mathfrak{a}_{1},\mathfrak{b}_{1})}=\frac{1}{\mathfrak{a}_{1}+1}\rho^{\mathfrak{b}_{1}}(1)-\frac{\mathfrak{b}_{1}}{2a_{1}\delta(\mathfrak{a}_{1}+1)}\int_{0}^{1}r^{\mathfrak{a}_{1}+1}\rho^{\mathfrak{b}_{1}-\delta+1}(v-u)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq C(\mathfrak{a}_{1},\mathfrak{b}_{1})\Big(\big|\chi_{\frac{1}{2}}^{\sharp}\rho\big|_{\infty}^{\mathfrak{b}_{1}}+\big(\int_{0}^{1}r^{\frac{p\mathfrak{a}_{1}+p-2}{p-1}}\rho^{\frac{p\mathfrak{b}_{1}-\gamma}{p-1}+1-\delta}\,\mathrm{d}r\big)^{\frac{p-1}{p}}\big|\chi_{1}^{\flat}(r^{2}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1})^{\frac{1}{p}}(u,v)\big|_{p}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(p,\mathfrak{a}_{1},\mathfrak{b}_{1})\Big(1+\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{1}}\int_{0}^{1}r^{\frac{p\mathfrak{a}_{1}+p-2}{p-1}}\rho^{\frac{p\mathfrak{b}_{1}-\gamma}{p-1}+1-\delta}\,\mathrm{d}r\Big)+\varepsilon_{1}\big|\chi_{1}^{\flat}(r^{2}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1})^{\frac{1}{p}}(u,v)\big|_{p}^{p}.

Next, for
j\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
, define the following two sequences
\{\mathfrak{a}_{j}\}_{j=1}^{\infty}
and
\{\mathfrak{b}_{j}\}_{j=1}^{\infty}

as

\mathfrak{a}_{j+1}=\frac{p}{p-1}\mathfrak{a}_{j}+\frac{p-2}{p-1},\quad\,\,\mathfrak{b}_{j+1}=\frac{p}{p-1}\mathfrak{b}_{j}-\frac{\gamma}{p-1}+1-\delta.

Certainly, we can solve for
(\mathfrak{a}_{j+1},\mathfrak{b}_{j+1})
from the above that, for
j\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
,

(4.25)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{a}_{j+1}

\displaystyle=2(p-1)\big(\frac{p}{p-1}\big)^{j}-p+2,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{b}_{j+1}

\displaystyle=(\gamma-2\delta+1)(p-1)\big(\frac{p}{p-1}\big)^{j}+\gamma+(p-1)(\delta-1).

Then (
4.24
) becomes

\mathcal{I}_{(\mathfrak{a}_{1},\mathfrak{b}_{1})}\leq C(p,\mathfrak{a}_{1},\mathfrak{b}_{1})\Big(1+\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{1}}\mathcal{I}_{(\mathfrak{a}_{2},\mathfrak{b}_{2})}\Big)+\varepsilon_{1}\big|\chi_{1}^{\flat}(r^{2}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1})^{\frac{1}{p}}(u,v)\big|_{p}^{p},

and we can follow a calculation similar to (
4.24
) to obtain that,
for all
j\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
and
\varepsilon_{j}\in(0,1)
,

(4.26)

\mathcal{I}_{(\mathfrak{a}_{j},\mathfrak{b}_{j})}\leq C(p,\mathfrak{a}_{j},\mathfrak{b}_{j})\Big(1+\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{j}}\mathcal{I}_{(\mathfrak{a}_{j+1},\mathfrak{b}_{j+1})}\Big)+\varepsilon_{j}\big|\chi_{1}^{\flat}(r^{2}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1})^{\frac{1}{p}}(u,v)\big|_{p}^{p}.

Define the constants

\varepsilon_{0}:=1,\qquad C(p,\mathfrak{a}_{0},\mathfrak{b}_{0}):=1.

(
4.26
) implies that, for
j\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
,

(4.27)

\displaystyle I_{3,1}=\mathcal{I}_{(\mathfrak{a}_{1},\mathfrak{b}_{1})}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{j}\Big(\prod_{l=0}^{k-1}\frac{C(p,\mathfrak{a}_{l},\mathfrak{b}_{l})}{\varepsilon_{l}}\Big)C(p,\mathfrak{a}_{k},\mathfrak{b}_{k})+\Big(\prod_{l=1}^{j}\frac{C(p,\mathfrak{a}_{l},\mathfrak{b}_{l})}{\varepsilon_{l}}\Big)\mathcal{I}_{(\mathfrak{a}_{j+1},\mathfrak{b}_{j+1})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{j}\Big(\prod_{l=0}^{k-1}\frac{C(p,\mathfrak{a}_{l},\mathfrak{b}_{l})}{\varepsilon_{l}}\Big)\varepsilon_{k}\big|\chi_{1}^{\flat}(r^{2}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1})^{\frac{1}{p}}(u,v)\big|_{p}^{p}.

To estimate
\mathcal{I}_{(\mathfrak{a}_{j+1},\mathfrak{b}_{j+1})}
, we see that, for each
p\in[2,\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta))
,
\delta\in(7-2\sqrt{10},1)
, and
\gamma\in(1,6\delta-3)
,
both
\{\mathfrak{a}_{j}\}_{j=1}^{\infty}
and
\{\mathfrak{b}_{j}\}_{j=1}^{\infty}
given in (
4.25
) are strictly increasing as
j\to\infty
.
Moreover, for these
(p,\gamma,\delta)
,

(4.28)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathfrak{a}_{j+1}}{\mathfrak{b}_{j+1}}\geq\frac{1}{2\delta-1}-\frac{1}{\mathfrak{b}_{j+1}}

\displaystyle\iff\frac{2(p-1)(\frac{p}{p-1})^{j}-p+3}{(\gamma-2\delta+1)(p-1)(\frac{p}{p-1})^{j}+\gamma+(p-1)(\delta-1)}\geq\frac{1}{2\delta-1}

\displaystyle\iff\gamma\leq 6\delta-3-\frac{(3\delta-2)p+1-\delta}{(p-1)(\frac{p}{p-1})^{j}+1}:=f_{p,\delta}(j).

Here, sequence
\{f_{p,\delta}(j)\}_{j=1}^{\infty}
satisfies that, for each
p\in[2,\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta))
and
\delta\in(7-2\sqrt{10},1)
,

f_{p,\delta}(j)\text{ is strictly increasing as $j\to\infty$},\qquad\lim_{j\to\infty}f_{p,\delta}(j)=6\delta-3>1.

Hence, for each
\gamma\in(1,6\delta-3)
, we can choose
j=j_{0}
to be sufficiently large such that
\eqref{635}_{3}
holds,
which in turn yields
\eqref{635}_{1}
.
As a consequence, fixing this constant
j_{0}
(depending only on
(p,\gamma,\delta)
),
we obtain from Lemma
4.4
that

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{(\mathfrak{a}_{j_{0}+1},\mathfrak{b}_{j_{0}+1})}=\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{\mathfrak{a}_{j_{0}+1}}\rho^{\mathfrak{b}_{j_{0}+1}}\,\mathrm{d}r\leq C(p).

Substituting
\mathcal{I}_{(\mathfrak{a}_{j_{0}+1},\mathfrak{b}_{j_{0}+1})}
into (
4.27
) with
j=j_{0}
yields

(4.29)

\displaystyle I_{3,1}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{j_{0}}\Big(\prod_{l=0}^{k-1}\frac{C(p,\mathfrak{a}_{l},\mathfrak{b}_{l})}{\varepsilon_{l}}\Big)C(p,\mathfrak{a}_{k},\mathfrak{b}_{k})+\Big(\prod_{l=1}^{j_{0}}\frac{C(p,\mathfrak{a}_{l},\mathfrak{b}_{l})}{\varepsilon_{l}}\Big)C(p)

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{j_{0}}\Big(\prod_{l=0}^{k-1}\frac{C(p,\mathfrak{a}_{l},\mathfrak{b}_{l})}{\varepsilon_{l}}\Big)\varepsilon_{k}\big|\chi_{1}^{\flat}(r^{2}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1})^{\frac{1}{p}}(u,v)\big|_{p}^{p}.

To further reduce inequality (
4.29
), for
\tilde{\varepsilon}\in(0,1)
sufficiently small,
we set

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{k}

\displaystyle=\frac{\tilde{\varepsilon}}{j_{0}}\,\prod_{l=0}^{k-1}\frac{\varepsilon_{l}}{C(p,\mathfrak{a}_{l},\mathfrak{b}_{l})}=\big(\frac{\tilde{\varepsilon}}{j_{0}}\big)^{2^{k-1}}\prod_{l=0}^{k-1}\frac{1}{C(p,\mathfrak{a}_{l},\mathfrak{b}_{l})^{2^{k-l-1}}}\qquad\text{for $1\leq k\leq j_{0}$}.

Then it follows from the above, (
4.29
), and Lemma
4.4
that

(4.30)

\displaystyle I_{3,1}

\displaystyle\leq C(p,\tilde{\varepsilon})+\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{j_{0}}\frac{\tilde{\varepsilon}}{j_{0}}\Big)\big|\chi_{1}^{\flat}(r^{2}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1})^{\frac{1}{p}}(u,v)\big|_{p}^{p}

\displaystyle\leq C(p,\tilde{\varepsilon})+\tilde{\varepsilon}\big(\big|(r^{2}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1})^{\frac{1}{p}}v\big|_{p}^{p}+\big|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{2}{\gamma-2\delta+1}}\rho\big|_{\infty}^{\gamma-2\delta+1}|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{p}}u|_{p}^{p}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(p,\tilde{\varepsilon})+\tilde{\varepsilon}\big|(r^{m}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1})^{\frac{1}{p}}v\big|_{p}^{p}+C_{0}\tilde{\varepsilon}\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{p}}u\big|_{p}^{p},

where we have also used the fact that
\frac{2}{\gamma-2\delta+1}>\frac{1}{2\delta-1}
whenever
\gamma\in(1,6\delta-3)
.

Thus, for both cases
n=2
and
n=3
, collecting (
4.22
)–(
4.23
) and (
4.30
), then choosing
\tilde{\varepsilon}
small enough such that

0<\tilde{\varepsilon}<\min\big\{\frac{a_{1}c_{p}}{16C(p)},\frac{\varepsilon}{C(p)},1\big\}\qquad\text{with $\varepsilon\in(0,1)$},

we conclude that, for all
\varepsilon\in(0,1)
,

(4.31)

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a_{1}c_{p}}{8}\big|(r^{m}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}|u|^{\frac{p-2}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u\big|_{2}^{2}+C(p,\varepsilon)\big(1+\big|(r^{m}\rho)^{\frac{1}{p}}u\big|_{p}^{p}\big)+\varepsilon\big|(r^{m}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1})^{\frac{1}{p}}v\big|_{p}^{p}.

Finally, substituting (
4.31
) into (
4.21
) implies the desired estimate.
∎

In addition, we show the following
L^{p}(I)
-estimates for
(r^{m}\rho)^{\frac{1}{p}}v
.

Lemma 4.8
.

Let
\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta)
be defined as in
Lemma
4.5
. Then, for all
p\in[2,\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta))
, there exists a constant
C(p)>0
such that

(4.32)

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|(r^{m}\rho)^{\frac{1}{p}}v\big|_{p}^{p}+\frac{pA\gamma}{4a_{1}\delta}\big|(r^{m}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1})^{\frac{1}{p}}v\big|_{p}^{p}\leq C(p)\big(\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{p}}u\big|_{p}^{p}+\big|(r^{m}\rho)^{\frac{1}{p}}u\big|_{p}^{p}\big).

Proof.

Let
\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta)
be defined as in Lemma
4.5
. First, multiplying (
4.14
) by
r^{m}\rho|v|^{p-2}v
with
p\in[2,\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta))
, along with
\eqref{e1.5}_{1}
, gives

(4.33)

\frac{1}{p}(r^{m}\rho|v|^{p})_{t}+\frac{1}{p}\big(r^{m}\underline{\rho u|v|^{p}}_{:=\mathcal{B}_{3}}\big)_{r}+\frac{A\gamma}{2a_{1}\delta}r^{m}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1}|v|^{p}=\frac{A\gamma}{2a_{1}\delta}r^{m}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1}uv|v|^{p-2}.

Next, we show that
r^{m}\mathrm{D}_{r}\mathcal{B}_{3}\in L^{1}(I)
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
so that we can apply Lemma
A.3

to obtain

(4.34)

\int_{0}^{\infty}(r^{m}\mathcal{B}_{3})_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r=0.

Indeed, based on (
4.7
)–(
4.8
), we have

r^{m}\rho\in L^{1}(I),\quad r^{\frac{m}{2}}u\in L^{2}(I),\quad r^{\frac{m}{n}}(\rho^{\delta-1})_{rr}\in L^{n}(I),\quad(\rho,u,\mathrm{D}_{r}u,(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r})\in L^{\infty}(I)

for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
. Thus, we obtain from the Hölder inequality that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |r^{m}\mathrm{D}_{r}\mathcal{B}_{3}|_{1}&\leq C(p)\big(\big|r^{m-1}\rho|u|^{p+1}\big|_{1}+\big|r^{m-1}\rho u|(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}|^{p}\big|_{1}+\big|r^{m}\rho^{2-\delta}(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}|u|^{p+1}\big|_{1}\big)\\
&\quad+C(p)\big(\big|r^{m}\rho^{2-\delta}u|(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}|^{p+1}\big|_{1}+\big|r^{m}\rho|u|^{p}u_{r}\big|_{1}+\big|r^{m}\rho u_{r}|(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}|^{p}\big|_{1}\big)\\
&\quad+C(p)\big|r^{m}\rho u|(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}|^{p-1}|(\rho^{\delta-1})_{rr}|\big|_{1}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\quad\hskip 5.69054pt\begin{aligned} &\leq C(p)|r^{m}\rho|_{1}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}|(u,(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r})|_{\infty}^{p}+C(p)|r^{m}\rho|_{1}|\rho^{1-\delta}|_{\infty}|(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}|_{\infty}|u|_{\infty}^{p+1}\\
&\quad+C(p)|r^{m}\rho|_{1}|\rho|_{\infty}^{1-\delta}|(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}|_{\infty}^{p+1}|u|_{\infty}\\
&\quad+C(p)|(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}|_{\infty}^{p-1}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}|r^{m}\rho|_{1}^{\frac{n-2}{2n}}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\frac{n+2}{2n}}|r^{\frac{m}{n}}(\rho^{\delta-1})_{rr}|_{n}<\infty.\end{aligned}

Now, integrating (
4.33
) over
I
, then we see from (
4.34
), Lemmas
4.3
–
4.4
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|(r^{m}\rho)^{\frac{1}{p}}v\big|_{p}^{p}+\frac{A\gamma}{2a_{1}\delta}\big|(r^{m}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1})^{\frac{1}{p}}v\big|_{p}^{p}\leq\frac{A\gamma}{2a_{1}\delta}\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1}uv|v|^{p-2}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq\frac{A\gamma}{4a_{1}\delta}\big|(r^{m}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1})^{\frac{1}{p}}v\big|_{p}^{p}+\frac{A\gamma}{4a_{1}\delta}\big(\big|\chi_{1}^{\flat}(r^{m}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1})^{\frac{1}{p}}u\big|_{p}^{p}+\big|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}(r^{m}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1})^{\frac{1}{p}}u\big|_{p}^{p}\big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{A\gamma}{4a_{1}\delta}\big|(r^{m}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1})^{\frac{1}{p}}v\big|_{p}^{p}+\frac{A\gamma}{4a_{1}\delta}\big|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{2}{\gamma-2\delta+1}}\rho\big|_{\infty}^{\gamma-2\delta+1}\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{p}}u\big|_{p}^{p}

\displaystyle+\frac{A\gamma}{4a_{1}\delta}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}\rho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-\delta}\big|(r^{m}\rho)^{\frac{1}{p}}u\big|_{p}^{p}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{A\gamma}{4a_{1}\delta}\big|(r^{m}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1})^{\frac{1}{p}}v\big|_{p}^{p}+C(p)\big(\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{p}}u\big|_{p}^{p}+\big|(r^{m}\rho)^{\frac{1}{p}}u\big|_{p}^{p}\big),

where we have also used Lemma
4.4
with the facts that

\frac{2}{\gamma-2\delta+1}>\begin{cases}0\quad&\text{if $n=2$},\\
\frac{1}{2\delta-1}\quad&\text{if $n=3$ \ and \ $\gamma\in(1,6\delta-3)$}.\end{cases}

This completes the proof of Lemma
4.8
.
∎

Now, combining Lemmas
4.7
–
4.8
, we give the proof for Lemma
4.5
.

Proof for Lemma
4.5
.

Let
p\in[2,\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta))
. First, multiplying both sides of (
4.32
) by
\frac{8a_{1}\delta\varepsilon}{A\gamma p}

with
\varepsilon\in(0,1)
, we arrive at

\frac{8a_{1}\delta\varepsilon}{A\gamma p}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|(r^{m}\rho)^{\frac{1}{p}}v\big|_{p}^{p}+2\varepsilon\big|(r^{m}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1})^{\frac{1}{p}}v\big|_{p}^{p}\leq C(p)\varepsilon\big(\big|(r^{m}\rho)^{\frac{1}{p}}u\big|_{p}^{p}+\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{p}}u\big|_{p}^{p}\big).

Then summing the above inequality with (
4.17
) yields

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big(\frac{1}{p}\big|(r^{m}\rho)^{\frac{1}{p}}u\big|_{p}^{p}+\frac{8a_{1}\delta\varepsilon}{A\gamma p}\big|(r^{m}\rho)^{\frac{1}{p}}v\big|_{p}^{p}\Big)+a_{1}c_{p}\big|(r^{m}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}|u|^{\frac{p-2}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u\big|_{2}^{2}+\varepsilon\big|(r^{m}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1})^{\frac{1}{p}}v\big|_{p}^{p}

\displaystyle\leq C(p)\big(1+\big|(r^{m}\rho)^{\frac{1}{p}}u\big|_{p}^{p}\big)+C(p,\varepsilon)+C(p)\varepsilon\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{p}}u\big|_{p}^{p}.

As a consequence, we can set

\varepsilon=\min\big\{\frac{a_{1}c_{p}}{100C(p)},\frac{1}{2}\big\},

and then apply the Grönwall inequality to the resulting inequality to obtain
the desired estimate. For the
L^{p}(I)
-boundedness of
(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{p}}(u_{0},v_{0})
, we see from Lemmas
A.1
,
A.10
, and
B.1
that

\displaystyle\big|(r^{m}\rho_{0})^{\frac{1}{p}}(u_{0},v_{0})\big|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq|r^{m}\rho_{0}|_{1}^{\frac{1}{p}}|(u_{0},(\rho_{0}^{\delta-1})_{r})|_{\infty}\leq C_{0}\|\rho_{0}\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{1}{p}}\|(\boldsymbol{u}_{0},\nabla\rho_{0}^{\delta-1})\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C(p).

This completes the proof.
∎

4.4.
Uniform Upper Bound of the Density

Lemma 4.9
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

|\rho(t)|_{\infty}\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

By Lemma
4.3
, it remains to establish the uniform upper bound of
\rho
near the origin:

(4.35)

|\chi_{1}^{\flat}\rho(t)|_{\infty}\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

First, it follows from Lemma
4.4
that, for both the 2-D case and 3-D case,

(4.36)

|\chi_{1}^{\flat}\rho(t)|_{2\delta-1}\leq C_{0}\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Next, note that
\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta)>n
due to Lemma
4.6
. Thus, for any fixed
p_{0}
satisfying

n<p_{0}<\min\big\{\frac{1}{1-\delta},\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta)\big\},

we obtain from (
4.9
), (
4.36
), Lemmas
4.5
and
A.1
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

\displaystyle|\chi_{1}^{\flat}\rho|_{\infty}^{2\delta-1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}(\rho^{2\delta-1},\rho^{\delta}(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r})|_{1}\leq C_{0}+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}\rho^{\delta}(u,v)|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{-\frac{m}{p_{0}-1}}|_{1}^{\frac{p_{0}-1}{p_{0}}}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}\rho|_{\infty}^{\frac{\delta p_{0}-1}{p_{0}}}|(r^{m}\rho)^{\frac{1}{p_{0}}}(u,v)|_{p_{0}}\leq C(T)+\frac{1}{20}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}\rho|_{\infty}^{2\delta-1},

where we have also used the fact that
0<\frac{\delta p_{0}-1}{p_{0}}<2\delta-1
.

Therefore, we obtain (
4.35
), and hence the global uniform upper bound for
\rho
.
∎

5.
Global Uniform Bound of the Effective Velocity

This section is devoted to establishing the global uniform
L^{\infty}(I)
-estimate of the effective velocity
v
in spherical coordinates. We first establish the following
L^{2}(I)
-estimate for
\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}u
.

Lemma 5.1
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

|\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}u(t)|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\Big(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}|v|_{\infty}^{2}+1\Big)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

Multiplying both sides of
\eqref{e1.5}_{2}
by
u
, along with
\eqref{e1.5}_{1}
and (
4.9
), leads to

(5.1)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(\rho u^{2})_{t}+2a_{1}\rho^{\delta}\Big(\delta u_{r}^{2}+(\delta-1)m\frac{uu_{r}}{r}+\frac{m}{2}\frac{u^{2}}{r^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle=\Big(\underline{2a_{1}\delta\rho^{\delta}uu_{r}+a_{1}(2\delta-1)m\frac{\rho^{\delta}u^{2}}{r}-\frac{1}{2}\rho u^{3}-A\rho^{\gamma}u}_{:=\mathcal{B}_{4}}\Big)_{r}+A\rho^{\gamma}u_{r}-\frac{m}{2}\frac{\rho vu^{2}}{r}.

Here we need to show that
\mathcal{B}_{4}\in W^{1,1}(I)
and
\mathcal{B}_{4}|_{r=0}=0
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
, which allows us to apply Lemma
A.3
to obtain

(5.2)

\int_{0}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}_{4})_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r=-\mathcal{B}_{4}|_{r=0}=0.

This can be seen as follows:
On one hand,
\mathcal{B}_{4}|_{r=0}=0
follows directly
from (
4.9
),
u|_{r=0}=0
,
and the fact that
(\rho,u,\mathrm{D}_{r}u)\in C(\bar{I})
for each
t\in(0,T]
due to (
4.8
).
On the other hand, if
n=2

(m=1)
, (
4.7
)–(
4.8
)
imply

\displaystyle r\rho\in L^{1}(I),\quad\sqrt{r}(u,\mathrm{D}_{r}u,\rho^{\frac{\delta-1}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u,\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u)\in L^{2}(I),\quad(\rho,\mathrm{D}_{r}u,u_{r},(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r})\in L^{\infty}(I)

for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
, so that

\displaystyle|\rho|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq|\chi_{1}^{\flat}\rho|_{1}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}\rho|_{1}\leq|\rho|_{\infty}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{-1}|_{\infty}|r\rho|_{1}<\infty,

\displaystyle|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|\chi_{1}^{\flat}\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}\leq|\rho|_{\infty}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{-\frac{1}{2}}|_{\infty}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}\sqrt{r}\rho^{\frac{\delta-1}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}<\infty,

which, along with the Hölder inequality, yields

\displaystyle\quad\,\begin{aligned} |\mathcal{B}_{4}|_{1}&\leq C_{0}\big(|\rho^{\delta}u\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{1}+\big|\rho|u|^{3}\big|_{1}+|\rho^{\gamma}u|_{1}\big)\\
&\leq C_{0}\big(|\rho|_{\infty}^{\delta}|\sqrt{r}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}+|\rho|_{\infty}|u|_{\infty}|\sqrt{r}u|_{2}|\sqrt{r}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}+|r\rho|_{1}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}\big)<\infty,\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |(\mathcal{B}_{4})_{r}|_{1}&\leq C_{0}\big|\big(\rho(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}u\mathrm{D}_{r}u,\rho^{\delta}u\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u,\rho^{\delta}u_{r}\mathrm{D}_{r}u\big)\big|_{1}\\
&\quad+C_{0}\big|\big(\rho^{2-\delta}(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}u^{3},\rho u^{2}u_{r}\big)\big|_{1}+C_{0}\big|\big(\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1}(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}u,\rho^{\gamma}u_{r}\big)\big|_{1}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\ \begin{aligned} &\leq C_{0}|\rho|_{\infty}|(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}|_{\infty}|\sqrt{r}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}+C_{0}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\delta}|\sqrt{r}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}|\sqrt{r}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}\\
&\quad+C_{0}|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}+C_{0}|\rho|_{1}\big(|\rho|_{\infty}^{1-\delta}|(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}|_{\infty}|u|_{\infty}^{3}+|u|_{\infty}^{2}|u_{r}|_{\infty}\big)\\
&\quad+C_{0}|\rho|_{1}\big(|\rho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-\delta}|(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}|_{\infty}|u|_{\infty}+|\rho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}|u_{r}|_{\infty}\big)<\infty.\end{aligned}

Furthermore, if
n=3

(m=2)
, (
4.7
)–(
4.8
) imply

(u,r\mathrm{D}_{r}u,r\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u)\in L^{2}(I),\quad(\rho,u,\mathrm{D}_{r}u,(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r})\in L^{\infty}(I),\quad r^{2}\rho\in L^{1}(I)

for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
, so that

\displaystyle|\rho|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq|\chi_{1}^{\flat}\rho|_{1}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}\rho|_{1}\leq|\rho|_{\infty}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{-2}|_{\infty}|r^{2}\rho|_{1}<\infty,\qquad|\rho^{\delta}|_{2}\leq|\rho|_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\delta-\frac{1}{2}}<\infty,

\displaystyle|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|\chi_{1}^{\flat}\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}\leq|\rho|_{\infty}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{-1}|_{\infty}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r\rho^{\frac{\delta-1}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}<\infty,

which, along with the Hölder inequality, yields

\displaystyle\quad\,\begin{aligned} |\mathcal{B}_{4}|_{1}&\leq C_{0}\big(|\rho^{\delta}u\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{1}+\big|\rho|u|^{3}\big|_{1}+|\rho^{\gamma}u|_{1}\big)\\
&\leq C_{0}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\frac{\delta}{2}}|u|_{2}|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}+C_{0}|\rho|_{1}\big(|u|_{\infty}^{3}+|\rho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}|u|_{\infty}\big)<\infty,\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |(\mathcal{B}_{4})_{r}|_{1}&\leq C_{0}\big|\big(\rho(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}u\mathrm{D}_{r}u,\rho^{\delta}u\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u,\rho^{\delta}u_{r}\mathrm{D}_{r}u\big)\big|_{1}\\
&\quad+C_{0}\big|\big(\rho^{2-\delta}(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}u^{3},\rho u^{2}u_{r}\big)\big|_{1}+C_{0}\big|\big(\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1}(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}u,\rho^{\gamma}u_{r}\big)\big|_{1}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\ \begin{aligned} &\leq C_{0}|\rho|_{2}|(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}|_{\infty}|u|_{2}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}+C_{0}|\rho^{\delta}|_{2}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}|r\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}\\
&\quad+C_{0}|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}+C_{0}|\rho|_{1}\big(|\rho|_{\infty}^{1-\delta}|(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}|_{\infty}|u|_{\infty}^{3}+|u|_{\infty}^{2}|u_{r}|_{\infty}\big)\\
&\quad+C_{0}|\rho|_{1}\big(|\rho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-\delta}|(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}|_{\infty}|u|_{\infty}+|\rho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}|u_{r}|_{\infty}\big)<\infty.\end{aligned}

Integrating (
5.1
) over
I
, we obtain from (
5.2
), Lemmas
4.3
and
4.9
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(5.3)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+\underline{2a_{1}\int_{0}^{\infty}\rho^{\delta}\Big(\delta u_{r}^{2}+(\delta-1)m\frac{uu_{r}}{r}+\frac{m}{2}\frac{u^{2}}{r^{2}}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r}_{:=I_{4}}

\displaystyle=A\int_{0}^{\infty}\rho^{\gamma}u_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r\ \underline{-\frac{m}{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\rho vu^{2}}{r}\,\mathrm{d}r}_{:=I_{5}}\leq C_{0}|\rho|_{2\gamma-\delta}^{\gamma-\frac{\delta}{2}}|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}+I_{5}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|\chi_{1}^{\flat}\rho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-\frac{\delta}{2}}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{-m}|_{\infty}^{\gamma-\frac{\delta}{2}}\big|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{m}\rho|_{2\gamma-\delta}^{\gamma-\frac{\delta}{2}}\big)|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}+I_{5}

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon,T)+\varepsilon|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}^{2}+I_{5}.

First, for
I_{4}
, according to the Young inequality

\Big|(\delta-1)mu_{r}\frac{u}{r}\Big|\leq m(\delta-1)^{2}u_{r}^{2}+\frac{m}{4}\frac{u^{2}}{r^{2}},

we can find a constant
c_{*}>0
, depending only on
(a_{1},n,\delta)
, such that

(5.4)

I_{4}\geq 2a_{1}\int_{0}^{\infty}\rho^{\delta}\Big((-m\delta^{2}+(2m+1)\delta-m)u_{r}^{2}+\frac{m}{4}\frac{u^{2}}{r^{2}}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r\geq c_{*}|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2},

where we have also used the fact that
-m\delta^{2}+(2m+1)\delta-m>0
for
\delta\in(\frac{m}{m+1},1)
.

For
I_{5}
, it follows from Lemma
4.9
and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

\displaystyle\text{if $n=2$:}\quad I_{5}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|v|_{\infty}|\rho|_{\infty}^{1-\delta}\Big|(r\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{u}{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T)|v|_{\infty}\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}u\big|_{2}^{2};

\displaystyle\text{if $n=3$:}\quad I_{5}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|v|_{\infty}|\rho|_{\infty}^{1-\delta}|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}u|_{2}\Big|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\frac{u}{r}\Big|_{2}\leq C(T)|v|_{\infty}^{2}\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}u\big|_{2}^{2}+\frac{c_{*}}{8}\Big|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\frac{u}{r}\Big|_{2}^{2},

which yields that, for both
n=2
and
n=3
,

(5.5)

I_{5}\leq C(T)(1+|v|_{\infty}^{2})\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}u\big|_{2}^{2}+\frac{c_{*}}{8}\Big|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\frac{u}{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}.

Thus, collecting (
5.3
)–(
5.5
) and choosing
\varepsilon
sufficiently small, we have

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+c_{*}|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T)+C(T)\big(1+|v|_{\infty}^{2}\big)\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{2}}u\big|_{2}^{2}.

Finally, integrating the above over
[0,t]
, together with Lemma
4.5
, yields the desired result. To check the
L^{2}(I)
-boundedness of
\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{0}
, we see from Lemmas
A.1
,
A.10
, and
B.1
that

\displaystyle|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{0}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|\chi_{1}^{\flat}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{0}|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{0}|_{2}\leq|\rho_{0}|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|u_{0}|_{\infty}+|\rho_{0}|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{-\frac{m}{2}}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{0}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\|\rho_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\big)\leq C_{0}\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{H^{2}}\leq C_{0}.

This completes the proof of Lemma
5.1
.
∎

Next, we aim to derive the
L^{2}([0,T];L^{\infty}(I))
-estimate for
\rho^{1-\delta}u
. We first need an auxiliary lemma associated with
parameter
\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta)
.

Lemma 5.2
.

Let
\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta)
be defined as in
Lemma
4.5
, and let
(\delta,\gamma)
satisfy (
4.2
).

(i)

If
n=2

(m=1)
, then

(5.6)

\delta<f^{\mathsf{a}}(\tilde{p}_{1}(\delta)),\qquad\delta<f^{\mathsf{b}}(\tilde{p}_{1}(\delta)),

with
f^{\mathsf{a}}(\tau):=\frac{2\tau-1}{2\tau+1}
and
f^{\mathsf{b}}(\tau):=\frac{2\tau+3}{2\tau+7};

(ii)

If
n=3

(m=2)
, then

(5.7)

\delta<f^{\mathsf{c}}(\tilde{p}_{2}(\delta)),\qquad\text{with }f^{\mathsf{c}}(\tau):=\frac{\tau+2}{\tau+4}.

Proof.

We divide the proof into two steps.

1. Proof for (
5.6
).
Since
\tilde{p}_{1}(\delta)>2
in view of Lemma
4.6
, a direct calculation gives

\displaystyle\delta<f^{\mathsf{a}}(\tilde{p}_{1}(\delta))

\displaystyle\iff 5\delta^{2}-1+4\delta\sqrt{2\delta-1}>0,

\displaystyle\delta<f^{\mathsf{b}}(\tilde{p}_{1}(\delta))

\displaystyle\iff 11\delta^{2}-10\delta+3+4\delta\sqrt{2\delta-1}>0,

which, of course, leads to (
5.6
), due to
\delta\in(\frac{1}{2},1)
.

2. Proof for (
5.7
).
Similarly, via a direct calculation, we have

\delta<f^{\mathsf{c}}(\tilde{p}_{2}(\delta))\iff(2\delta-1)(3\delta-2)+\sqrt{\delta(2\delta-1)(3\delta-2)}>0,

and the right-hand sides of the above hold automatically, due to
1>\delta>\frac{2}{3}>\frac{1}{2}
.
∎

As a direct consequence of Lemma
5.2
, we have

Lemma 5.3
.

Let
\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta)
be defined as in
Lemma
4.5
, and let
(\delta,\gamma)
satisfy (
4.2
). Then there exists a parameter
q\in(n,\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta))
such that

(i)

if
n=2

(m=1)
, then

(5.8)

\qquad(2q-1)-(2q+1)\delta\geq 0,\quad(2q+3)-(2q+7)\delta\geq 0,\quad(2q+1)-(2q+3)\delta>0;

(ii)

if
n=3

(m=2)
, then

(5.9)

(q+2)-(q+4)\delta\geq 0,\qquad q-(q+1)\delta\geq 0,\qquad(q+1)-(q+2)\delta>0.

Proof.

Based on Lemma
5.2
, since
\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta)>n
in view of Lemma
4.6
and
(f^{\mathsf{a}},f^{\mathsf{b}},f^{\mathsf{c}})(\tau)
are all strictly increasing with respect of
\tau
for
\tau\in(1,\infty)
, we can find
q\in(n,\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta))
, sufficiently closing to
\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta)
, such that

\delta\leq g(q)<g(\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta))\qquad\text{for function $g=f^{\mathsf{a}}$, $f^{\mathsf{b}}$, or $f^{\mathsf{c}}$}.

Therefore, we can derive from the above that such a parameter
q
satisfies, if
n=2
(
m=1
),

\displaystyle(2q-1)-(2q+1)\delta\geq 0,\quad(2q+3)-(2q+7)\delta\geq 0,

\displaystyle(2q+1)-(2q+3)\delta=(2q-1)-(2q+1)\delta+2(1-\delta)>0;

if
n=3

(m=2)
,

\displaystyle(q+2)-(q+4)\delta\geq 0,

\displaystyle q-(q+1)\delta=(q+2)-(q+4)\delta+(3\delta-2)>0,

\displaystyle(q+1)-(q+2)\delta=(q+2)-(q+4)\delta+(2\delta-1)>0.

This completes the proof.
∎

Now, we establish the
L^{2}([0,T];L^{\infty}(I))
-estimate for
\rho^{1-\delta}u
.

Lemma 5.4
.

For any
\varepsilon\in(0,1)
, there exists a constant
C(\varepsilon,T)>0
such that

\int_{0}^{t}|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(\varepsilon,T)\Big(1+\int_{0}^{t}|v|_{\infty}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)+\varepsilon\sup_{s\in[0,t]}|v|_{\infty}^{2}\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

We divide the proof into three steps.

1.
Let the parameter
q\in(n,\tilde{p}_{m}(\delta))
be chosen as in Lemma
5.3
. First, by (
4.9
), Lemma
A.3
, and the Hölder inequality, we have

(5.10)

\displaystyle|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}^{q}

\displaystyle\leq|(\rho^{q(1-\delta)}|u|^{q})_{r}|_{1}\leq C_{0}\big(\big|\rho^{(q+1)(1-\delta)}|u|^{q}(v,u)\big|_{1}+\big|\rho^{q(1-\delta)}|u|^{q-1}u_{r}\big|_{1}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|v|_{\infty}\big|\rho^{(q+1)(1-\delta)}u^{q}\big|_{1}+C_{0}|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}\big|\rho^{q(1-\delta)}u^{q}\big|_{1}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}^{\frac{q-2}{2}}\big|\rho^{(q+2)-(q+3)\delta}u^{q}\big|_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}:=\sum_{i=6}^{8}I_{i}.

2.
If
n=2

(m=1)
, by Lemmas
4.5
,
4.9
, and
5.3
, and the Young inequality, we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} I_{6}&\leq C_{0}|v|_{\infty}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\frac{(2q+1)-(2q+3)\delta}{2}}\big|(r^{-1}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}^{\frac{q}{2}}\big|(r\rho)^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}^{\frac{q}{2}}\leq C_{0}|v|_{\infty}\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}^{\frac{q}{2}},\end{aligned}

(5.11)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} I_{7}&\leq C_{0}|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\frac{(2q-1)-(2q+1)\delta}{2}}\big|(r^{-1}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}^{\frac{q}{2}}\big|(r\rho)^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}^{\frac{q}{2}}\\
&\leq C(T)\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}^{\frac{q^{2}}{2q-2}}+\frac{1}{20}|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}^{q},\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} I_{8}&\leq C_{0}|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}^{\frac{q-2}{2}}|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\frac{(2q+3)-(2q+7)\delta}{4}}\big|(r^{-1}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}^{\frac{q}{4}}\big|(r\rho)^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}^{\frac{q}{4}}\\
&\leq C(T)|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}^{\frac{2q}{q+2}}\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}^{\frac{q^{2}}{2q+4}}+\frac{1}{20}|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}^{q}.\end{aligned}

Combining (
5.10
)–(
5.11
), we arrive at the estimate of form:

|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}^{2}\leq C(T)\Big(|v|_{\infty}^{\frac{2}{q}}\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}+\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}^{\frac{q}{q-1}}+|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}^{\frac{4}{q+2}}\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}^{\frac{q}{q+2}}\Big).

Since
q>2
, integrating the above over
[0,t]
, along with Lemmas
4.5
and
5.1
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities, yields that, for all
\varepsilon\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\int_{0}^{t}\Big(|v|_{\infty}^{\frac{2}{q}}\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}+\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}^{\frac{q}{q-1}}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+C(T)\Big(\int_{0}^{t}|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)^{\frac{2}{q+2}}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)^{\frac{q}{q+2}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon,T)\int_{0}^{t}\big(|v|_{\infty}^{2}+\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}^{q}+1\big)\,\mathrm{d}s+\varepsilon\sup_{s\in[0,t]}|v|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon,T)\Big(1+\int_{0}^{t}|v|_{\infty}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)+\varepsilon\sup_{s\in[0,t]}|v|_{\infty}^{2}.

3.
If
n=3

(m=2)
, we can similarly obtain from Lemmas
4.9
and
5.3
, and the Young inequality that

(5.12)

\displaystyle I_{6}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|v|_{\infty}|\rho|_{\infty}^{(q+1)-(q+2)\delta}|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{q}}u|_{q}^{q}\leq C(T)|v|_{\infty}\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}^{q},

\displaystyle I_{7}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}|\rho|_{\infty}^{q-(q+1)\delta}|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{q}}u|_{q}^{q}\leq C(T)\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}^{\frac{q^{2}}{q-1}}+\frac{1}{20}|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}^{q},

\displaystyle I_{8}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}^{\frac{q-2}{2}}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\frac{(q+2)-(q+4)\delta}{2}}|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{q}}u|_{q}^{\frac{q}{2}}|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}^{\frac{2q}{3}}+\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}^{\frac{q^{2}}{q-1}}\big)+\frac{1}{20}|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}^{q}.

Combining (
5.10
) and (
5.12
), we arrive at the estimate of form:

|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}^{2}\leq C(T)\Big(|v|_{\infty}^{\frac{2}{q}}\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}^{2}+\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}^{\frac{2q}{q-1}}+|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}^{\frac{4}{3}}\Big).

Since
q>3
, integrating the above over
[0,t]
, along with Lemmas
4.5
and
5.1
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities, yields that, for all
\varepsilon\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\int_{0}^{t}\Big(|v|_{\infty}^{\frac{2}{q}}\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}^{2}+\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}^{\frac{2q}{q-1}}+|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}^{\frac{4}{3}}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\int_{0}^{t}\big(|v|_{\infty}^{2}+\big|(r^{m-2}\rho^{\delta})^{\frac{1}{q}}u\big|_{q}^{q}+1\big)\,\mathrm{d}s+C(T)\Big(\int_{0}^{t}|\rho^{\frac{\delta}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)^{\frac{2}{3}}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(1+\int_{0}^{t}|v|_{\infty}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)+C(T)\Big(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}|v|_{\infty}^{2}\Big)^{\frac{4}{3}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon,T)\Big(1+\int_{0}^{t}|v|_{\infty}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)+\varepsilon\sup_{s\in[0,t]}|v|_{\infty}^{2}.

This completes the proof of Lemma
5.4
.
∎

Finally, the uniform
L^{\infty}(I)
-estimate of
v
can be derived as follows.

Lemma 5.5
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

|v(t)|_{\infty}\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

First, define the flow map
X(t,r):[0,T]\times I\to I
as

X_{t}(t,r)=u(t,X(t,r))\qquad\text{with}\quad X(0,r)=r.

Then (
4.14
), together with the above, implies the following ODE:

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}v(t,X(t,r))+\frac{A\gamma}{2a_{1}\delta}(\rho^{\gamma-\delta}v)(t,X(t,r))=\frac{A\gamma}{2a_{1}\delta}(\rho^{\gamma-\delta}u)(t,X(t,r)),

which, along with the method of characteristic, yields

(5.13)

\displaystyle v(t,X(t,r))

\displaystyle=v_{0}(r)\exp\Big(-\int_{0}^{t}\frac{A\gamma}{2a_{1}\delta}\rho^{\gamma-\delta}(\tau,X(\tau,r))\,\mathrm{d}\tau\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{A\gamma}{2a_{1}\delta}\int_{0}^{t}(\rho^{\gamma-\delta}u)(s,X(s,r))\ \exp\Big(\!-\!\int_{s}^{t}\frac{A\gamma}{2a_{1}\delta}\rho^{\gamma-\delta}(\tau,X(\tau,r))\,\mathrm{d}\tau\Big)\,\mathrm{d}s.

Notice that
\rho\geq 0
, and
v_{0}
is uniformly bounded owing to Lemmas
A.1
,
A.10
, and
B.1
:

|v_{0}|_{\infty}\leq C_{0}|(u_{0},(\rho_{0}^{\delta-1})_{r})|_{\infty}\leq C_{0}\|(\boldsymbol{u}_{0},\nabla(\rho_{0}^{\delta-1}))\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C_{0}(\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{H^{2}}+1)\leq C_{0}.

Then (
5.13
), together with the above, Lemmas
4.9
and
5.4
, and the Hölder inequality, yields that, for any
\varepsilon\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in[0,t]}|v|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big(|v_{0}|_{\infty}+\int_{0}^{t}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-1}|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}\mathrm{d}s\Big)\leq C_{0}+C(T)\Big(\int_{0}^{t}|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}+C(T)\Big(C(\varepsilon,T)\Big(1+\int_{0}^{t}|v|_{\infty}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)+\varepsilon\sup_{s\in[0,t]}|v|_{\infty}^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}},

which leads to

(5.14)

\sup_{s\in[0,t]}|v|_{\infty}^{2}\leq C(\varepsilon,T)\big(1+\int_{0}^{t}|v|_{\infty}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\big)+C(T)\varepsilon\sup_{s\in[0,t]}|v|_{\infty}^{2},

where we have used the fact that
\sup_{s\in[0,t]}|v|_{\infty}^{2}=\big(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}|v|_{\infty}\big)^{2}
.

Finally, choosing
\varepsilon
in (
5.14
) sufficiently small and then applying the Grönwall inequality provide the desired result.
∎

6.
Non-Formation of Cavitation
inside the Fluids in Finite Time

This section is devoted to showing that the cavitation does not form inside the fluids in finite
time and establishing the lower-bound estimate for the density.

6.1.
Refined
\rho
-Weighted Estimates for the Velocity

First, by Lemmas
5.4
–
5.5
, we can directly obtain the
L^{2}([0,T];L^{\infty}(I))
-estimate of
\rho^{1-\delta}u
.

Lemma 6.1
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

\int_{0}^{t}|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Next, we show the refined
\rho
-weighted estimates for
u
.

Lemma 6.2
.

Let

(6.1)

\alpha=\max\big\{0,\frac{5\delta-3}{2}\big\}.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{5}}u(t)|_{5}^{5}+\int_{0}^{t}|\rho^{\frac{\delta-\alpha}{2}}|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for any $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

Let
\alpha
be given as in (
6.1
). We divide the proof into three steps.

1.
L^{2}
-estimate for
\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}u
.
We first see from
\eqref{e1.5}_{1}
that

(6.2)

(\rho^{1-\alpha})_{t}+(u\rho^{1-\alpha})_{r}-\alpha\rho^{1-\alpha}u_{r}+m(1-\alpha)\frac{\rho^{1-\alpha}u}{r}=0.

Next, multiplying
\eqref{e1.5}_{2}
by
\rho^{-\alpha}u
, together with (
4.9
) and (
6.2
), gives

(6.3)

\!\!\!\!\begin{aligned} &\,\big(\frac{1}{2}\rho^{1-\alpha}|u|^{2}\big)_{t}+2a_{1}\delta\rho^{\delta-\alpha}\Big(u_{r}^{2}-m\frac{1-\delta}{\delta-\alpha}u_{r}\frac{u}{r}+\frac{m}{2}\frac{1-\alpha}{\delta-\alpha}\frac{u^{2}}{r^{2}}\Big)\\
&=\Big(\underline{a_{1}\delta\rho^{\delta-\alpha}\big(2uu_{r}+m\frac{2\delta-\alpha-1}{\delta-\alpha}\frac{u^{2}}{r}\big)-\frac{\alpha+3}{6}\rho^{1-\alpha}u|u|^{2}}_{:=\mathcal{B}_{5}}\Big)_{r}\\
&\quad\!-\frac{A\gamma}{2a_{1}\delta}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1-\alpha}(v-u)u+\rho^{1-\alpha}vu\Big(\alpha u_{r}-\frac{m(1-\alpha)}{2}\frac{u}{r}\Big)+\frac{\alpha(1-\alpha)}{12a_{1}\delta}\rho^{2-\delta-\alpha}(v-u)u^{3}\!.\end{aligned}

Here, for the detailed derivation of (
6.3
), see Appendix
D
. We need to show that
\mathcal{B}_{5}\in W^{1,1}(I)
and
\mathcal{B}_{5}|_{r=0}=0
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
, so that we can apply Lemma
A.3
to obtain

(6.4)

\int_{0}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}_{5})_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r=-\mathcal{B}_{5}|_{r=0}=0.

Indeed, on one hand,
\mathcal{B}_{5}|_{r=0}=0
follows from
u|_{r=0}=0
and
(\rho,u,\mathrm{D}_{r}u)\in C(\bar{I})
for
t\in(0,T]
due to (
4.8
). On the other hand, we see from (
4.7
)–(
4.8
) that, for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
,

(\rho,(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r},u,\mathrm{D}_{r}u)\in L^{\infty}(I),\qquad r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u,\frac{\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u}{r}\big)\in L^{2}(I),

which implies that, for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
,

(6.5)

\displaystyle|(u,\mathrm{D}_{r}u)|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|\chi_{1}^{\flat}(u,\mathrm{D}_{r}u)|_{2}+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}(u,u_{r})|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|(u,\mathrm{D}_{r}u)|_{\infty}+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{-\frac{m}{2}}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u,u_{r})|_{2}<\infty.

Then it follows from (
6.5
),
\alpha<\delta<1
, and the Hölder inequality that

\displaystyle\quad\,|\mathcal{B}_{5}|_{1}\leq C_{0}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\delta-\alpha}|u|_{2}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}+C_{0}|\rho|_{\infty}^{1-\alpha}|u|_{\infty}|u|_{2}^{2}<\infty,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |(\mathcal{B}_{5})_{r}|_{1}&\leq C_{0}|\rho^{1-\alpha}(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}u\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{1}+C_{0}|\rho^{\delta-\alpha}u_{r}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{1}+C_{0}|\rho^{\delta-\alpha}u\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{1}\\
&\quad+C_{0}\big|\rho^{2-\delta-\alpha}(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}u^{3}\big|_{1}+C_{0}|\rho^{1-\alpha}u^{2}u_{r}|_{1}\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\ \begin{aligned} &\leq C_{0}\big(|\rho|_{\infty}^{1-\alpha}|(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}|_{\infty}|u|_{2}+|\rho|_{\infty}^{\delta-\alpha}|u_{r}|_{2}\big)|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}+C_{0}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\delta-\alpha}|r^{\frac{2-m}{2}}u|_{2}|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}\\
&\quad+C_{0}|\rho|_{\infty}^{2-\delta-\alpha}|(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}|_{\infty}|u|_{\infty}|u|_{2}^{2}+C_{0}|\rho|_{\infty}^{1-\alpha}|u|_{2}^{2}|u_{r}|_{\infty}<\infty.\end{aligned}

Based on (
6.4
), integrating (
6.3
) over
I
, we obtain

(6.6)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+\underline{2a_{1}\delta\int_{0}^{\infty}\rho^{\delta-\alpha}\Big(u_{r}^{2}-m\frac{1-\delta}{\delta-\alpha}u_{r}\frac{u}{r}+\frac{m}{2}\frac{1-\alpha}{\delta-\alpha}\frac{u^{2}}{r^{2}}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r}_{:=I_{9}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{A\gamma}{2a_{1}\delta}\int_{0}^{\infty}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1-\alpha}(v-u)u\,\mathrm{d}r+\int_{0}^{\infty}\rho^{1-\alpha}vu\Big(\alpha u_{r}+\frac{m(\alpha-1)}{2}\frac{u}{r}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle+\frac{\alpha(1-\alpha)}{12a_{1}\delta}\int_{0}^{\infty}\rho^{2-\delta-\alpha}(v-u)u^{3}\,\mathrm{d}r:=\sum_{i=10}^{12}I_{i}.

To derive the lower bound for
I_{9}
, let
X=\sqrt{2a_{1}\delta}\rho^{\frac{\delta-\alpha}{2}}u_{r}
and
Y=\sqrt{2a_{1}\delta}\rho^{\frac{\delta-\alpha}{2}}\frac{u}{r}
. Then write
I_{9}
in view of
(X,Y)
as

I_{9}=\int_{0}^{\infty}\Big(X^{2}-m\frac{1-\delta}{\delta-\alpha}XY+\frac{m}{2}\frac{1-\alpha}{\delta-\alpha}Y^{2}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r.

Clearly, the integrand in the above is a quadratic form with respect to
(X,Y)
and its discriminant
\mathscr{D}
is strictly negative:

\mathscr{D}=m^{2}\frac{(1-\delta)^{2}}{(\delta-\alpha)^{2}}-2m\frac{1-\alpha}{\delta-\alpha}<0.

Hence, there exists a constant
c_{*1}>0
, which depends only on
(n,\delta)
, such that

(6.7)

I_{9}\geq c_{*1}\int_{0}^{\infty}(X^{2}+Y^{2})\,\mathrm{d}r=2a_{1}\delta c_{*1}|\rho^{\frac{\delta-\alpha}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}.

We continue to estimate
I_{10}
–
I_{12}
. It follows from Lemmas
4.3
,
4.9
, and
5.5
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(6.8)

\displaystyle I_{10}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-\delta}|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+C_{0}\big|\rho^{\gamma-\delta+\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}v\big|_{2}|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}u|_{2}\leq C(T)\big(|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+\big|\rho^{\gamma-\delta+\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}v\big|_{2}^{2}\big),

\displaystyle I_{11}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\frac{1-\delta}{2}}|v|_{\infty}|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}u|_{2}|\rho^{\frac{\delta-\alpha}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}\leq C(T)|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+\frac{a_{1}\delta c_{*1}}{8}|\rho^{\frac{\delta-\alpha}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle I_{12}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\rho^{2-\delta-\alpha}vu^{3}|_{1}\leq C_{0}|v|_{\infty}|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T)\big(1+|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}^{2}\big)|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}u|_{2}^{2},

where, in
I_{12}
, we have also used the facts that
0\leq\alpha<1
and

-\frac{\alpha(1-\alpha)}{12a_{1}\delta}\int_{0}^{\infty}\rho^{2-\delta-\alpha}u^{4}\,\mathrm{d}r\leq 0.

Therefore, collecting (
6.6
)–(
6.8
) yields

(6.9)

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+a_{1}\delta c_{*1}|\rho^{\frac{\delta-\alpha}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T)(1+|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}^{2})|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+C(T)\big|\rho^{\gamma-\delta+\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}v\big|_{2}^{2}.

2. Estimate for
v
.
We multiply (
4.14
) by
\rho^{2(\gamma-\delta)+(1-\alpha)}v
and obtain from
\eqref{e1.5}_{1}
that

(6.10)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\big(\rho^{2(\gamma-\delta)+(1-\alpha)}v^{2}\big)_{t}+\frac{1}{2}\big(\underline{u\rho^{2(\gamma-\delta)+(1-\alpha)}v^{2}}_{:=\mathcal{B}_{6}}\big)_{r}+\frac{A\gamma}{2a_{1}\delta}\rho^{3(\gamma-\delta)+(1-\alpha)}v^{2}

\displaystyle=\big(\frac{\alpha}{2}-(\gamma-\delta)\big)\rho^{2(\gamma-\delta)+(1-\alpha)}v^{2}u_{r}-\big((\gamma-\delta)+\frac{1-\alpha}{2}\big)m\rho^{2(\gamma-\delta)+(1-\alpha)}v^{2}\frac{u}{r}

\displaystyle+\frac{A\gamma}{2a_{1}\delta}\rho^{3(\gamma-\delta)+(1-\alpha)}vu.

We need to show that
\mathcal{B}_{6}\in W^{1,1}(I)
and
\mathcal{B}_{6}|_{r=0}=0

for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
, which allows us to apply Lemma
A.3
to obtain

(6.11)

\int_{0}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}_{6})_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r=-\mathcal{B}_{6}|_{r=0}=0.

Indeed, on one hand, to obtain
\mathcal{B}_{6}|_{r=0}=0
, we first note that
v=u+\frac{2a_{1}\delta}{\delta-1}(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}
and

(u,(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r})\in L^{\infty}(I)\qquad\text{for {\it a.e.} $t\in(0,T)$}

due to (
4.7
)–(
4.8
), which implies

(6.12)

v\in L^{\infty}(I)\qquad\text{for {\it a.e.} $t\in(0,T)$}.

Then it follows from
u|_{r=0}
and
(\rho,u)\in C(\bar{I})
for each
t\in(0,T]
(due to (
4.8
)) that
\mathcal{B}_{6}|_{r=0}=0
.
On the other hand, it follows from (
4.7
)–(
4.8
) and (
6.5
) that

\displaystyle(u,\mathrm{D}_{r}u)\in L^{2}(I),\quad(\rho,u,u_{r},(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r})\in L^{\infty}(I),

\displaystyle r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u,u_{r},\mathrm{D}_{r}(\rho^{\gamma-1})_{r})\in L^{2}(I),\quad r^{\frac{m}{n}}(\rho^{\delta-1})_{rr}\in L^{n}(I)

for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
. Thus, we obtain from Lemma
A.4
that

\displaystyle|(\rho^{\gamma-1})_{r}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\big|\chi_{1}^{\flat}(\rho^{\gamma-1})_{r}\big|_{2}+\big|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}(\rho^{\gamma-1})_{r}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{2-m}{2}}|_{\infty}\big|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}(\rho^{\gamma-1})_{r}\big|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{-\frac{m}{2}}|_{\infty}\big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\rho^{\gamma-1})_{r}\big|_{2}<\infty,

which, along with (
4.9
), (
6.12
), and the Hölder and Young inequalities, yields

\displaystyle\quad\,|\mathcal{B}_{6}|_{1}\leq C_{0}|u|_{\infty}\big(|\rho|_{\infty}^{2(\gamma-\delta)+(1-\alpha)}|u|_{2}^{2}+|\rho|_{\infty}^{1-\alpha}|(\rho^{\gamma-1})_{r}|_{2}^{2}\big)<\infty,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |(\mathcal{B}_{6})_{r}|_{1}&\leq C_{0}\big|\big(u_{r}\rho^{2(\gamma-\delta)+(1-\alpha)}v^{2},u\rho^{\gamma-2\delta+2-\alpha}(\rho^{\gamma-1})_{r}v^{2},u\rho^{2(\gamma-\delta)+(1-\alpha)}vv_{r}\big)\big|_{1}\\
&\leq C_{0}|u_{r}|_{\infty}\big(|\rho|_{\infty}^{2(\gamma-\delta)+(1-\alpha)}|u|_{2}^{2}+|\rho|_{\infty}^{1-\alpha}|(\rho^{\gamma-1})_{r}|_{2}^{2}\big)\\
&\quad+C_{0}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-2\delta+2-\alpha}|(\rho^{\gamma-1})_{r}|_{2}|u|_{2}|v|_{\infty}^{2}\\
&\quad+C_{0}|\rho|_{\infty}^{2(\gamma-\delta)+(1-\alpha)}|v|_{\infty}\big(|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r(u_{r},(\rho^{\delta-1})_{rr})|_{2}+|u|_{2}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}(u_{r},(\rho^{\delta-1})_{rr})|_{2}\big)\\
&\leq C_{0}|u_{r}|_{\infty}\big(|\rho|_{\infty}^{2(\gamma-\delta)+(1-\alpha)}|u|_{2}^{2}+|\rho|_{\infty}^{1-\alpha}|(\rho^{\gamma-1})_{r}|_{2}^{2}\big)\\
&\quad+C_{0}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-2\delta+2-\alpha}|(\rho^{\gamma-1})_{r}|_{2}|u|_{2}|v|_{\infty}^{2}\\
&\quad+C_{0}|\rho|_{\infty}^{2(\gamma-\delta)+(1-\alpha)}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}|v|_{\infty}\big(|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{2-m}{2}}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{1}{n}}|_{n^{*}}|r^{\frac{m}{n}}(\rho^{\delta-1})_{rr}|_{n}\big)\\
&\quad+C_{0}|\rho|_{\infty}^{2(\gamma-\delta)+(1-\alpha)}|u|_{2}|v|_{\infty}\big(|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{-\frac{m}{2}}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{-\frac{m}{n}}|_{n^{*}}|r^{\frac{m}{n}}(\rho^{\delta-1})_{rr}|_{n}\big)<\infty,\end{aligned}

where
n^{*}
is the parameter defined in §
3.1.3
.

Now, integrating (
6.10
) over
I
, we obtain from (
6.11
), Lemmas
4.9
and
5.5
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(6.13)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big|\rho^{\gamma-\delta+\frac{1}{2}(1-\alpha)}v\big|_{2}^{2}+\frac{A\gamma}{2a_{1}\delta}\big|\rho^{\frac{3}{2}(\gamma-\delta)+\frac{1}{2}(1-\alpha)}v\big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\int_{0}^{\infty}\rho^{2(\gamma-\delta)+(1-\alpha)}v^{2}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|\,\mathrm{d}r+C_{0}\int_{0}^{\infty}\rho^{3(\gamma-\delta)+(1-\alpha)}|v||u|\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big|\rho^{\gamma-\delta+\frac{1}{2}(1-\alpha)}v\big|_{2}\Big(|\rho|_{\infty}^{\frac{2\gamma+1-3\delta}{2}}|v|_{\infty}|\rho^{\frac{\delta-\alpha}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}+|\rho|_{\infty}^{2\gamma-2\delta}|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}u|_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\big|\rho^{\gamma-\delta+\frac{1}{2}(1-\alpha)}v\big|_{2}^{2}+|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}\big)+\frac{a_{1}\delta c_{*1}}{8}|\rho^{\frac{\delta-\alpha}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}.

Combining (
6.13
) with (
6.9
) gives

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\big(|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+\big|\rho^{\gamma-\delta+\frac{1}{2}(1-\alpha)}v\big|_{2}^{2}\big)+\frac{a_{1}\delta c_{*1}}{2}|\rho^{\frac{\delta-\alpha}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}^{2}\big)|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+C(T)\big|\rho^{\gamma-\delta+\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}v\big|_{2}^{2},

which, along with the Grönwall inequality and Lemma
6.1
, yields that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

(6.14)

|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}u(t)|_{2}^{2}+\big|\rho^{\gamma-\delta+\frac{1}{2}(1-\alpha)}v(t)\big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}|\rho^{\frac{\delta-\alpha}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T),

where the
L^{2}(I)
-bound of
(\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}u_{0},\rho_{0}^{\gamma-\delta+\frac{1}{2}(1-\alpha)}v_{0})
follows from Lemmas
A.1
,
A.10
, and
B.1
:

\displaystyle|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}u_{0}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|\rho_{0}|_{\infty}^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}\big(|\chi_{1}^{\flat}u_{0}|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}u_{0}|_{2}\big)\leq C_{0}\big(|u_{0}|_{\infty}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{-\frac{m}{2}}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{0}|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\big)\leq C_{0}\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{H^{2}}\leq C_{0},

\displaystyle\big|\rho_{0}^{\gamma-\delta+\frac{1}{2}(1-\alpha)}v_{0}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|\rho_{0}|_{\infty}^{\gamma-\delta}|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}u_{0}|_{2}+C_{0}|\rho_{0}|_{\infty}^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}|(\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1})_{r}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(1+|\chi_{1}^{\flat}(\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1})_{r}|_{\infty}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{-\frac{m}{2}}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1})_{r}|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(1+\|\nabla\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\nabla\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}\|_{L^{2}}\big)\leq C_{0}\big(1+\|\nabla\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1}\|_{H^{2}}\big)\leq C_{0}.

3.
L^{5}
-estimates for
\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{5}}u
.
Multiplying
\eqref{e1.5}_{2}
by
\rho^{-\alpha}|u|^{\ell-2}u
with
\ell=3,4,5
, together with (
4.9
) and (
6.2
), gives

\displaystyle\,\big(\frac{1}{\ell}\rho^{1-\alpha}|u|^{\ell}\big)_{t}+2(\ell-1)a_{1}\delta\rho^{\delta-\alpha}|u|^{\ell-2}\Big(u_{r}^{2}-m\frac{1-\delta}{\delta-\alpha}u_{r}\frac{u}{r}+\frac{m}{\ell}\frac{1-\alpha}{\delta-\alpha}\frac{u^{2}}{r^{2}}\Big)

(6.15)

\displaystyle=\Big(\underline{a_{1}\delta\rho^{\delta-\alpha}|u|^{\ell-2}\big(2uu_{r}+2m\frac{\ell\delta-\ell\alpha-\ell+1}{\ell(\delta-\alpha)}\frac{u^{2}}{r}\big)-\frac{(\ell-1)\alpha+\ell+1}{\ell(\ell+1)}\rho^{1-\alpha}u|u|^{\ell}}_{:=\mathcal{B}_{7}}\Big)_{r}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{A\gamma}{2a_{1}\delta}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1-\alpha}(v-u)|u|^{\ell-2}u+\rho^{1-\alpha}vu|u|^{\ell-2}\Big(\alpha u_{r}-\frac{m(1-\alpha)}{\ell}\frac{u}{r}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(\ell-1)\alpha(1-\alpha)}{2\ell(\ell+1)a_{1}\delta}\rho^{2-\delta-\alpha}(v-u)u|u|^{\ell}.

The detailed derivation of (
6.15
) is given in Appendix
D
. Then we need to show that
\mathcal{B}_{7}\in W^{1,1}(I)
and
\mathcal{B}_{7}|_{r=0}=0
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
, so that we can apply Lemma
A.3
to obtain

(6.16)

\int_{0}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}_{7})_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r=-\mathcal{B}_{7}|_{r=0}=0.

Note that, this proof can be derived similarly from that of (
6.4
) in Step 1, since
u\in C(\bar{I})
for
t\in(0,T]
due to (
4.8
), and
|\mathcal{B}_{7}|+|(\mathcal{B}_{7})_{r}|\leq C(\ell)|u|^{\ell-2}(|\mathcal{B}_{5}|+|(\mathcal{B}_{5})_{r}|)
.

Hence, based on (
6.16
), integrating (
6.15
) over
I
, we have

(6.17)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\ell}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{\ell}}u|_{\ell}^{\ell}+\underline{2(\ell-1)a_{1}\delta\int_{0}^{\infty}\rho^{\delta-\alpha}|u|^{\ell-2}\Big(u_{r}^{2}-m\frac{1-\delta}{\delta-\alpha}u_{r}\frac{u}{r}+\frac{m}{\ell}\frac{1-\alpha}{\delta-\alpha}\frac{u^{2}}{r^{2}}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r}_{:=I_{13}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{A\gamma}{2a_{1}\delta}\int_{0}^{\infty}\rho^{\gamma-\delta+1-\alpha}(v-u)|u|^{\ell-2}u\,\mathrm{d}r+\int_{0}^{\infty}\rho^{1-\alpha}vu|u|^{\ell-2}\Big(\alpha u_{r}+\frac{m(\alpha-1)}{\ell}\frac{u}{r}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle+\frac{(\ell-1)\alpha(1-\alpha)}{2\ell(\ell+1)a_{1}\delta}\int_{0}^{\infty}\rho^{2-\delta-\alpha}(v-u)u|u|^{\ell}\,\mathrm{d}r:=\sum_{i=14}^{16}I_{i}.

To estimate
I_{13}
, we claim that there exists a constant
c_{*\ell}>0
, depending only on
(n,\delta,\ell)
, such that

(6.18)

\displaystyle I_{13}\geq c_{*\ell}\int_{0}^{\infty}(X^{2}+Y^{2})\,\mathrm{d}r=2a_{1}\delta(\ell-1)c_{*\ell}\Big(\big|\rho^{\frac{\delta-\alpha}{2}}|u|^{\frac{\ell-2}{2}}u_{r}\big|_{2}^{2}+\Big|\rho^{\frac{\delta-\alpha}{2}}|u|^{\frac{\ell-2}{2}}\frac{u}{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}\Big),

where
(X,Y)
are given by

X=\sqrt{2(\ell-1)a_{1}\delta}\rho^{\frac{\delta-\alpha}{2}}|u|^{\frac{\ell-2}{2}}u_{r},\qquad Y=\sqrt{2(\ell-1)a_{1}\delta}\rho^{\frac{\delta-\alpha}{2}}|u|^{\frac{\ell-2}{2}}\frac{u}{r}.

To this end, write
I_{13}
in view of
(X,Y)
as

I_{13}=\int_{0}^{\infty}\Big(X^{2}-m\frac{1-\delta}{\delta-\alpha}XY+\frac{m}{\ell}\frac{1-\alpha}{\delta-\alpha}Y^{2}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r.

Since the integrand in the above is a quadratic form with respect to
(X,Y)
and its discriminant
\mathscr{D}
is strictly negative:

\mathscr{D}=m^{2}\frac{(1-\delta)^{2}}{(\delta-\alpha)^{2}}-\frac{4m}{\ell}\frac{1-\alpha}{\delta-\alpha}<0,

we obtain claim (
6.18
).

To estimate
I_{14}
–
I_{16}
, it follows from the fact that
0\leq\alpha<1
, Lemmas
4.3
,
4.9
, and
5.5
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(6.19)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} I_{14}&\leq C_{0}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-\delta}|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{\ell}}u|_{\ell}^{\ell}+C_{0}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-\delta}|v|_{\infty}\big|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{\ell-1}}u\big|_{\ell-1}^{\ell-1}\leq C(T)\big(|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{\ell}}u|_{\ell}^{\ell}+|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{\ell-1}}u|_{\ell-1}^{\ell-1}\big),\\
I_{15}&\leq C(\ell)|\rho|_{\infty}^{\frac{1-\delta}{2}}|v|_{\infty}|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{\ell}}u|_{\ell}^{\frac{\ell}{2}}\big|\rho^{\frac{\delta-\alpha}{2}}|u|^{\frac{\ell-2}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u\big|_{2}\\
&\leq C(\ell,T)|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{\ell}}u|_{\ell}^{\ell}+\frac{a_{1}\delta c_{*\ell}}{8}\big|\rho^{\frac{\delta-\alpha}{2}}|u|^{\frac{\ell-2}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u\big|_{2}^{2},\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} I_{16}&\leq C(\ell)|\rho^{2-\delta-\alpha}v|u|^{\ell+1}|_{1}\leq C(\ell)|v|_{\infty}|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{\ell}}u|_{\ell}^{\ell}\\
&\leq C(\ell,T)\big(1+|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}^{2}\big)|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{\ell}}u|_{\ell}^{\ell},\end{aligned}

where we have used the following fact in
I_{16}
:

-\frac{(\ell-1)\alpha(1-\alpha)}{2\ell(\ell+1)a_{1}\delta}\int_{0}^{\infty}\rho^{2-\delta-\alpha}|u|^{\ell+2}\,\mathrm{d}r\leq 0.

Therefore, collecting (
6.17
)–(
6.19
) yields

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{\ell}}u|_{\ell}^{\ell}+a_{1}\delta c_{*\ell}\big|\rho^{\frac{\delta-\alpha}{2}}|u|^{\frac{\ell-2}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u\big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(\ell,T)\big(1+|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}^{2}\big)|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{\ell}}u|_{\ell}^{\ell}+C(T)|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{\ell-1}}u|_{\ell-1}^{\ell-1}.

Applying the Grönwall inequality, we can iteratively obtain from (
6.14
) and Lemma
6.1
that, for
\ell=3,4,5
and any
t\in[0,T]
,

|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{\ell}}u(t)|_{\ell}^{\ell}+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\rho^{\frac{\delta-\alpha}{2}}|u|^{\frac{\ell-2}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(\ell,T).

Here, for the
L^{\ell}(I)
-boundedness of
\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\alpha}{\ell}}u_{0}
, we see from Lemmas
A.1
,
A.10
, and
B.1
that

\displaystyle|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\alpha}{\ell}}u_{0}|_{\ell}

\displaystyle\leq|\rho_{0}|_{\infty}^{\frac{1-\alpha}{\ell}}\big(|\chi_{1}^{\flat}u_{0}|_{\ell}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}u_{0}|_{\ell}\big)\leq C(\ell)\big(|u_{0}|_{\infty}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{-\frac{m}{\ell}}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{\ell}}u_{0}|_{\ell}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(\ell)\big(\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{\ell}}\big)\leq C(\ell)\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{H^{2}}\leq C(\ell).

This completes the proof.
∎

Based on Lemma
6.2
, we have the following
L^{2}([0,T];L^{\infty}(I))
-estimate for
\rho^{\frac{1-\delta}{2}}u
.

Lemma 6.3
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

\int_{0}^{t}|\rho^{\frac{1-\delta}{2}}u|_{\infty}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

First, let
\alpha
be given as in (
6.1
). Then we have

2\alpha-5\delta+3\geq 0.

Next, we obtain from the above, (
4.9
), Lemmas
4.9
,
5.5
,
6.2
, and
A.3
, and the Hölder inequality that

\displaystyle|\rho^{\frac{1-\delta}{2}}u|_{\infty}^{5}

\displaystyle\leq\big|(\rho^{\frac{5(1-\delta)}{2}}|u|^{5})_{r}\big|_{1}\leq C_{0}\big(\big|\rho^{\frac{5(1-\delta)}{2}}|u|^{3}uu_{r}\big|_{1}+\big|\rho^{\frac{7}{2}(1-\delta)}(v-u)u^{5}\big|_{1}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\frac{2\alpha-5\delta+3}{2}}\big(|\rho|_{\infty}^{\frac{1-\delta}{2}}|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{5}}u|_{5}^{\frac{5}{2}}\big|\rho^{\frac{\delta-\alpha}{2}}|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}u_{r}\big|_{2}+|v|_{\infty}|\rho|_{\infty}^{1-\delta}|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{5}}u|_{5}^{5}\big)

\displaystyle+C_{0}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\frac{2\alpha-5\delta+3}{2}}|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}|\rho^{\frac{1-\alpha}{5}}u|_{5}^{5}\leq C(T)\big(\big|\rho^{\frac{\delta-\alpha}{2}}|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}u_{r}\big|_{2}+|\rho^{1-\delta}u|_{\infty}+1\big).

Consequently, integrating the above over
[0,t]
, together with Lemmas
6.1
–
6.2
, leads to the desired estimate.
∎

Finally, we can obtain the
L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{2}(I))
-estimate and
L^{4}([0,T];L^{\infty}(I))
-estimate for
u
.

Lemma 6.4
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

|(\rho^{\gamma-\delta}v,u)(t)|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}(|\rho^{\frac{\delta-1}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}+|u|_{\infty}^{4})\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for any $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

We divide the proof into four steps.

1.

First, multiplying
\eqref{e1.5}_{2}
by
\rho^{-1}u
, along with (
4.9
), gives

(6.20)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(u^{2})_{t}+a_{1}\delta\rho^{\delta-1}\big(2u_{r}^{2}+m\frac{u^{2}}{r^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle=\Big(\underline{2a_{1}\delta\rho^{\delta-1}u_{r}u+a_{1}\delta m\rho^{\delta-1}\frac{u^{2}}{r}}_{:=\mathcal{B}_{8}}\ \underline{-\frac{2}{3}u^{3}}_{:=\mathcal{B}_{9}}\Big)_{r}-\frac{A\gamma}{2a_{1}\delta}\rho^{\gamma-\delta}(v-u)u

\displaystyle+vu_{r}u-\frac{(1-\delta)m}{2}(v-u)\frac{u^{2}}{r}.

2.
We show that
\mathcal{B}_{8}\in L^{\frac{n}{n-1}}(I)\cap D^{1,1}(I)
,
\mathcal{B}_{9}\in W^{1,1}(I)
, and
(\mathcal{B}_{8}+\mathcal{B}_{9})|_{r=0}=0
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
, which allows us to apply Lemma
A.3
to obtain

(6.21)

\int_{0}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}_{8})_{r}+(\mathcal{B}_{9})_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r=-(\mathcal{B}_{8}+\mathcal{B}_{9})|_{r=0}=0.

\mathcal{B}_{9}\in W^{1,1}(I)
and
(\mathcal{B}_{8}+\mathcal{B}_{9})|_{r=0}=0
follow from
u|_{r=0}=0

and the facts that
\rho>0
,
u\in L^{2}(I)
, and
(\rho,u,u_{r})\in C(\bar{I})
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
due to (
4.7
)–(
4.8
) and (
6.5
). Hence, it remains to prove
\mathcal{B}_{8}\in L^{\frac{n}{n-1}}(I)\cap D^{1,1}(I)
. Let

X_{1}:=\rho^{\delta-1}\nabla\boldsymbol{u}\cdot\boldsymbol{u},\qquad X_{2}:=\rho^{\delta-1}(\diver\boldsymbol{u})\boldsymbol{u}

On one hand, we see from Theorem
3.2
, Lemma
C.1
(since
X_{1}
and
X_{2}
are spherically symmetric vector functions), and the Hölder inequality that, for
i=1,2
and
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
,

\displaystyle\|X_{i}\|_{L^{\frac{n}{n-1}}}\leq C_{0}\|\nabla X_{i}\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(\|\nabla\rho^{\delta-1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\rho^{\delta-1}\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{2}}\big)\|\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C_{0}\|\rho^{\frac{\delta-1}{2}}\nabla\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}<\infty.

This, along with Lemma
B.1
, gives

(6.22)

\displaystyle r^{\frac{m(n-1)}{n}}\rho^{\delta-1}uu_{r}\in L^{\frac{n}{n-1}}(I),\quad r^{\frac{m(n-1)}{n}}\rho^{\delta-1}u\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\in L^{\frac{n}{n-1}}(I)

\displaystyle\implies r^{\frac{m(n-1)}{n}}\rho^{\delta-1}u\mathrm{D}_{r}u\in L^{\frac{n}{n-1}}(I)\qquad\text{for {a.e.} $t\in(0,T)$}.

On the other hand, it follows from (
4.7
)–(
4.8
) and (
6.5
) that, for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
,

\displaystyle\rho>0\ \ \text{on $I$},\qquad(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}\in L^{\infty}(I),

\displaystyle(\rho,u,\mathrm{D}_{r}u)\in L^{2}(I)\cap L^{\infty}(I),\quad r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u,\rho^{\delta-1}\frac{\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u}{r}\big)\in L^{2}(I),

which, along with (
6.22
) and the Hölder inequality, leads to

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |\mathcal{B}_{8}|_{\frac{n}{n-1}}&\leq C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}\rho^{\delta-1}u\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\frac{n}{n-1}}+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}\rho^{\delta-1}u\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\frac{n}{n-1}}\\
&\leq C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}\rho^{\delta-1}|_{\infty}|u|_{\infty}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{\frac{m(1-n)}{n}}|_{\infty}\big|r^{\frac{m(n-1)}{n}}\rho^{\delta-1}u\mathrm{D}_{r}u\big|_{\frac{n}{n-1}}<\infty,\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |(\mathcal{B}_{8})_{r}|_{1}&\leq C_{0}|(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}u\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{1}+C_{0}\Big|\rho^{\delta-1}\big((u_{r})^{2},u_{rr}u,u_{r}\frac{u}{r},u(\frac{u}{r})_{r}\big)\Big|_{1}\\
&\leq C_{0}\big(|(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}|_{\infty}|u|_{2}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}+|\rho^{\frac{\delta-1}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}+|r^{\frac{2-m}{2}}u|_{2}|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\rho^{\delta-1}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}\big)<\infty.\end{aligned}

3.
Integrating (
6.20
) over
I
, together with (
6.21
), yields

(6.23)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|u|_{2}^{2}+2a_{1}\delta|\rho^{\frac{\delta-1}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}^{2}+ma_{1}\delta\Big|\rho^{\frac{\delta-1}{2}}\frac{u}{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=-\frac{A\gamma}{2a_{1}\delta}\int_{0}^{\infty}\rho^{\gamma-\delta}(v-u)u\,\mathrm{d}r+\int_{0}^{\infty}vu_{r}u\,\mathrm{d}r-\frac{(1-\delta)m}{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}\!(v-u)\frac{u^{2}}{r}\,\mathrm{d}r:=\sum_{i=17}^{19}I_{i}.

It follows from Lemmas
4.9
,
5.5
, and
6.3
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(6.24)

\displaystyle I_{17}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|\rho^{\gamma-\delta}v|_{2}|u|_{2}+|\rho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-\delta}|u|_{2}^{2}\big)\leq C(T)|(\rho^{\gamma-\delta}v,u)|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle I_{18}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\frac{1-\delta}{2}}|v|_{\infty}|u|_{2}|\rho^{\frac{\delta-1}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}\leq\frac{a_{1}\delta}{8}|\rho^{\frac{\delta-1}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}^{2}+C(T)|u|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle I_{19}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|\rho|_{\infty}^{\frac{1-\delta}{2}}|v|_{\infty}+|\rho^{\frac{1-\delta}{2}}u|_{\infty}\big)|u|_{2}\Big|\rho^{\frac{\delta-1}{2}}\frac{u}{r}\Big|_{2}\leq\frac{a_{1}\delta}{8}\Big|\rho^{\frac{\delta-1}{2}}\frac{u}{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}+C(T)|u|_{2}^{2},

Combining (
6.23
) with (
6.24
) gives

(6.25)

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|u|_{2}^{2}+a_{1}\delta|\rho^{\frac{\delta-1}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T)|(\rho^{\gamma-\delta}v,u)|_{2}^{2}.

4.
For the
L^{2}(I)
-estimate of
\rho^{\gamma-\delta}v
,
we first multiply (
4.14
) by
\rho^{2\gamma-2\delta}v
and then obtain from
\eqref{e1.5}_{1}
that

(6.26)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(\rho^{2\gamma-2\delta}v^{2})_{t}+\frac{1}{2}\big(\underline{u\rho^{2\gamma-2\delta}v^{2}}_{:=\mathcal{B}_{10}}\big)_{r}+\frac{A\gamma}{2a_{1}\delta}\rho^{3\gamma-3\delta}v^{2}

\displaystyle=\big(\frac{1}{2}+\delta-\gamma\big)\rho^{2\gamma-2\delta}v^{2}u_{r}-(\gamma-\delta)m\rho^{2\gamma-2\delta}v^{2}\frac{u}{r}+\frac{A\gamma}{2a_{1}\delta}\rho^{3\gamma-3\delta}vu.

We need to show that
\mathcal{B}_{10}\in W^{1,1}(I)
and
\mathcal{B}_{10}|_{r=0}=0

for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
, which allows us to apply Lemma
A.3
to obtain

(6.27)

\int_{0}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}_{10})_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r=-\mathcal{B}_{10}|_{r=0}=0.

This proof can be achieved by basically following that of (
6.11
) (with
\alpha=1
) in the proof of Lemma
6.2
. We omit the details here for brevity.

Thus, integrating (
6.26
) over
I
, we obtain from (
6.27
), Lemmas
4.9
and
5.5
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(6.28)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|\rho^{\gamma-\delta}v|_{2}^{2}+\frac{A\gamma}{2a_{1}\delta}\big|\rho^{\frac{3\gamma-3\delta}{2}}v\big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}\rho^{2\gamma-2\delta}v^{2}\Big(\big(\frac{1}{2}+\delta-\gamma\big)u_{r}-(\gamma-\delta)m\frac{u}{r}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r+\frac{A\gamma}{2a_{1}\delta}\int_{0}^{\infty}\rho^{3\gamma-3\delta}vu\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\frac{2\gamma+1-3\delta}{2}}|v|_{\infty}|\rho^{\gamma-\delta}v|_{2}|\rho^{\frac{\delta-1}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}+C_{0}|\rho|_{\infty}^{2\gamma-2\delta}|\rho^{\gamma-\delta}v|_{2}|u|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)|(\rho^{\gamma-\delta}v,u)|_{2}^{2}+\frac{a_{1}\delta}{8}|\rho^{\frac{\delta-1}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}.

Combining (
6.25
) with (
6.28
) gives

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|(\rho^{\gamma-\delta}v,u)|_{2}^{2}+\frac{a_{1}\delta}{2}|\rho^{\frac{\delta-1}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T)|(\rho^{\gamma-\delta}v,u)|_{2}^{2}.

which, along with the Grönwall inequality, yields

(6.29)

|(\rho^{\gamma-\delta}v,u)(t)|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}|\rho^{\frac{\delta-1}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

We still needs to check the
L^{2}(I)
-boundedness of
(\rho_{0}^{\gamma-\delta}v_{0},u_{0})
.
Indeed, it follows from Lemmas
A.1
,
A.10
, and
B.1
that

\displaystyle|u_{0}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|\chi_{1}^{\flat}u_{0}|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}u_{0}|_{2}\leq|u_{0}|_{\infty}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{-\frac{m}{2}}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{0}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\big)\leq C_{0}\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{H^{2}}\leq C_{0},

\displaystyle|\rho_{0}^{\gamma-\delta}v_{0}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\rho_{0}^{\gamma-\delta}(u_{0},(\rho_{0}^{\delta-1})_{r})|_{2}\leq C_{0}\big(|\rho_{0}|_{\infty}^{\gamma-\delta}|u_{0}|_{2}+|(\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1})_{r}|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\rho|_{\infty}^{\gamma-\delta}+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{2-m}{2}}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}(\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1})_{r}|_{2}+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{-\frac{m}{2}}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1})_{r}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(\|\rho\|_{L^{\infty}}^{\gamma-\delta}+\|\nabla(\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1})\|_{H^{1}}\big)\leq C_{0}.

Finally, it follows from (
6.29
), Lemmas
4.9
and
A.3
, and the Hölder inequality that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|u|_{\infty}^{4}\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{t}|(u^{2})_{r}|_{1}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq 4\int_{0}^{t}|u|_{2}^{2}|u_{r}|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq 4\Big(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}|u|_{2}^{2}\Big)\Big(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}|\rho|_{\infty}^{1-\delta}\Big)\int_{0}^{t}|\rho^{\frac{\delta-1}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T).

This completes the proof.
∎

6.2.
Non-Formation of Cavitation Inside the Fluids

Now, with the help of Lemmas
5.5
and
6.4
, we can show the pointwise estimates of
\rho

in the domain containing the origin.

Lemma 6.5
.

Suppose that

(6.30)

\inf_{z\in[0,r]}\rho_{0}(z)=\underline{\rho}(r)>0\qquad\text{for $r>0$},

with
\underline{\rho}(r)
, defined on
I
, satisfying
\underline{\rho}(r)\to 0
as
r\to\infty
.
Then, for any
R>0
, there exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

\begin{gathered}\inf_{(t,r)\in[0,T]\times[0,R]}\rho(t,r)\geq\frac{C(T)^{-1}\underline{\rho}(R)}{\big(R^{\frac{1}{2-2\delta}}+1\big)(\underline{\rho}(R)+1)}.\end{gathered}

In particular, the cavitation does not form in
[0,T]\times\{\boldsymbol{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{n}:\,|\boldsymbol{x}|\leq R\}

for any
R>0
.

Proof.

First, it follows from Lemma
A.1
that, for all
R>0
and
t\in[0,T]
,

(6.31)

|\chi_{R}^{\flat}\rho^{\delta-1}(t)|_{\infty}\leq C_{0}\Big(\sqrt{\frac{1+R}{R}}|\chi_{R}^{\flat}\rho^{\delta-1}(t)|_{2}+|\chi_{R}^{\flat}(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}(t)|_{2}\Big).

Then, by (
4.9
) and Lemmas
5.5
and
6.4
, we obtain that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

(6.32)

|\chi_{R}^{\flat}(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}(t)|_{2}\leq C_{0}|\chi_{R}^{\flat}(v,u)(t)|_{2}\leq C_{0}\big(\sqrt{R}|v(t)|_{\infty}+|u(t)|_{2}\big)\leq C(T)(\sqrt{R}+1).

Next, multiplying
\eqref{e1.5}_{1}
by
\chi_{R}^{\flat}(2\delta-2)\rho^{2\delta-3}
and integrating over
I
, we have

(6.33)

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|\chi_{R}^{\flat}\rho^{\delta-1}|_{2}^{2}=-2\int_{0}^{R}u(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}\rho^{\delta-1}\,\mathrm{d}r-(2\delta-2)\int_{0}^{R}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\rho^{2\delta-2}\,\mathrm{d}r:=\sum_{i=20}^{21}I_{i}.

Then, for
I_{20}
–
I_{21}
, we obtain from (
6.31
)–(
6.32
) and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(6.34)

\displaystyle I_{20}

\displaystyle\leq 2|u|_{\infty}|\chi_{R}^{\flat}(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}|_{2}|\chi_{R}^{\flat}\rho^{\delta-1}|_{2}\leq C(T)(R+1)|u|_{\infty}^{2}+|\chi_{R}^{\flat}\rho^{\delta-1}|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle I_{21}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\rho^{\frac{\delta-1}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}|\chi_{R}^{\flat}\rho^{\delta-1}|_{2}|\chi_{R}^{\flat}\rho^{\delta-1}|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\frac{R+1}{R}\Big)^{\frac{1}{4}}|\rho^{\frac{\delta-1}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}|\chi_{R}^{\flat}\rho^{\delta-1}|_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}+C(T)(\sqrt{R}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}|\rho^{\frac{\delta-1}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}|\chi_{R}^{\flat}\rho^{\delta-1}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\frac{R+1}{R}+\sqrt{R}+1\Big)+\big(1+|\rho^{\frac{\delta-1}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}\big)|\chi_{R}^{\flat}\rho^{\delta-1}|_{2}^{2}.

Combining (
6.33
)–(
6.34
), along with Lemmas
6.4
and the Grönwall inequality, yields

(6.35)

\displaystyle|\chi_{R}^{\flat}\rho^{\delta-1}(t)|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(|\chi_{R}^{\flat}\rho_{0}^{\delta-1}|_{2}^{2}+\frac{R+1}{R}+\sqrt{R}+1\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(R|\chi_{R}^{\flat}\rho_{0}^{\delta-1}|_{\infty}^{2}+\frac{R+1}{R}+\sqrt{R}+1\Big).

Collecting (
6.31
)–(
6.32
) and (
6.35
), and letting
R_{0}\geq 1
be a fixed sufficiently large constant, we obtain that, for all
t\in[0,T]
and
R\geq R_{0}
,

|\chi_{R}^{\flat}\rho^{\delta-1}(t)|_{\infty}\leq C(T)(\sqrt{R}+1)\big(|\chi_{R}^{\flat}\rho_{0}^{\delta-1}|_{\infty}+1\big)\leq C(T)(\sqrt{R}+1)\big(\underline{\rho}^{\delta-1}(R)+1\big),

which, implies that, for all
(t,r)\in[0,T]\times[0,R]
and
R\geq R_{0}
,

\rho(t,r)\geq\frac{C(T)^{-1}\underline{\rho}(R)}{\big(R^{\frac{1}{2-2\delta}}+1\big)(\underline{\rho}(R)+1)}.

Finally, for
R\leq R_{0}
, it follows from Lemma
A.1
, (
6.31
)–(
6.32
), and (
6.35
) that

\displaystyle|\chi_{R}^{\flat}\rho^{\delta-1}(t)|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq|\chi_{R_{0}}^{\flat}\rho^{\delta-1}(t)|_{\infty}\leq C_{0}\Big(\sqrt{\frac{1+R_{0}}{R_{0}}}|\chi_{R_{0}}^{\flat}\rho^{\delta-1}(t)|_{2}+|\chi_{R_{0}}^{\flat}(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}(t)|_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|\chi_{R_{0}}^{\flat}\rho_{0}^{\delta-1}|_{\infty}+1\big)\leq C(T),

which implies that
\rho(t,r)\geq C(T)^{-1}
for all
(t,r)\in[0,T]\times[0,R]
and
R\leq R_{0}
.
∎

7.
Global Estimates for
Regular Solutions with Far-Field Vacuum

The goal of this section is to establish the global uniform estimates for the regular solutions when
\bar{\rho}=0
. Let
T>0
be any fixed time, and let
(\rho,u)(t,r)
be the regular solution of problem (
4.4
) in
[0,T]\times I

obtained in Theorem
4.1
. Moreover, throughout this section, we always assume that
(
2.2
) holds.

Next, we consider the enlarged system (
3.2
) in spherical coordinates.
Specifically, we introduce the following two important quantities:

(7.1)

\phi=\frac{A\gamma}{\gamma-1}\rho^{\gamma-1},\qquad\psi=\frac{a\delta}{\delta-1}(\phi^{2\iota})_{r}=\frac{\delta}{\delta-1}(\rho^{\delta-1})_{r}.

Then (
4.4
) can be rewritten as the problem for
(\phi,u,\psi)
in
[0,T]\times I
:

(7.2)

\begin{cases}\displaystyle\phi_{t}+u\phi_{r}+(\gamma-1)\phi\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)=0,\\[4.0pt]
\displaystyle u_{t}+uu_{r}+\phi_{r}=2a_{1}\delta a\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}+2a_{1}\psi\big(\delta u_{r}+m(\delta-1)\frac{u}{r}\big),\\[4.0pt]
\displaystyle\psi_{t}+u\psi_{r}+\big(\delta u_{r}+(\delta-1)m\frac{u}{r}\big)\psi+\delta a\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}=0,\\[4.0pt]
\displaystyle(\phi,u,\psi)|_{t=0}=(\phi_{0},u_{0},\psi_{0})=(\frac{A\gamma}{\gamma-1}\rho_{0}^{\gamma-1},u_{0},\frac{\delta}{\delta-1}(\rho_{0}^{\delta-1})_{r})\qquad\text{for $r\in I$},\\[4.0pt]
\displaystyle u|_{r=0}=0\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\,\text{for $t\in[0,T]$},\\[4.0pt]
\displaystyle(\phi,u)\to(0,0)\qquad\,\ \ \text{as $r\to\infty$}\qquad\text{for $t\in[0,T]$}.\end{cases}

Clearly, the effective velocity
v
and its equation (
4.14
) take the following forms, respectively:

(7.3)

\displaystyle v=u+2a_{1}\psi=u+\frac{2a_{1}\delta a}{\delta-1}(\phi^{2\iota})_{r}=u+\frac{2a_{1}\delta a}{\gamma-1}\phi^{2\iota-1}\phi_{r},

(7.4)

\displaystyle v_{t}+uv_{r}+\frac{\gamma-1}{2a_{1}\delta a}\phi^{1-2\iota}(v-u)=0.

7.1.
Some Auxiliary Lemmas

The following two lemmas concern div-curl estimates and weighted div-curl estimates for spherically symmetric functions in spherical coordinates, which are frequently used in our analysis.

Lemma 7.1
.

Let
p\in(1,\infty)
and
\boldsymbol{f}(\boldsymbol{x})=f(r)\frac{\boldsymbol{x}}{r}\in C_{\rm c}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
.
Then

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{j}\boldsymbol{f}\|_{L^{p}}\sim\hskip 0.85358pt|r^{\frac{m}{p}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{j}f|_{p}\sim\Big|r^{\frac{m}{p}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{j-1}\big(f_{r}+\frac{m}{r}f\big)\Big|_{p}\quad

\displaystyle\text{for $j=1,2$},

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{k}\boldsymbol{f}\|_{L^{p}}\sim|r^{\frac{m}{p}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{k}f|_{p}\sim\Big|r^{\frac{m}{p}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{k-2}\big(f_{r}+\frac{m}{r}f\big)_{r}\Big|_{p}\quad

\displaystyle\text{for $k=3,4$},

where
F_{1}\sim F_{2}
denotes
C^{-1}F_{1}\leq F_{2}\leq CF_{1}
for some constant
C>0
depending only on
(n,p)
.

Proof.

Let
\boldsymbol{f}(\boldsymbol{x})=f(r)\frac{\boldsymbol{x}}{r}
. First, since
\boldsymbol{f}
is curl-free, we have the following identity

\Delta\boldsymbol{f}=\nabla\diver\boldsymbol{f}\implies\nabla\boldsymbol{f}=-\nabla(-\Delta)^{-1}\nabla\diver\boldsymbol{f},

where
(-\Delta)^{-1}
is defined via the Fourier transform:

\big((-\Delta)^{-1}g\big)(\boldsymbol{x}):=\mathcal{F}^{-1}\big[\frac{1}{4\pi|\boldsymbol{\omega}|^{2}}\mathcal{F}[g](\boldsymbol{\omega})\big](\boldsymbol{x}),

with
\mathcal{F}[g](\boldsymbol{\omega})
as
the Fourier transform of
g\in C^{\infty}_{\rm c}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
:
\mathcal{F}[g](\boldsymbol{\omega})=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}g(\boldsymbol{x})e^{-2\pi\mathrm{i}\boldsymbol{x}\cdot\boldsymbol{\omega}}\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x}
.

Thus, by the Mihlin–Hörmander multiplier theorem (see
[
24
, Theorem 6.2.7]
), we have

\|\nabla^{j}\diver\boldsymbol{f}\|_{L^{p}}\sim\|\nabla^{j+1}\boldsymbol{f}\|_{L^{p}}\qquad\text{for integer $0\leq j\leq 3$},

which, together with
\diver\boldsymbol{f}=f_{r}+\frac{m}{r}f
and Lemma
B.1
, leads to the desired results.
∎

Lemma 7.2
.

Let
\boldsymbol{f}(t,\boldsymbol{x})=f(t,r)\frac{\boldsymbol{x}}{r}\in C_{\mathrm{c}}^{\infty}([0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{n})
. Then, for any
q\geq 2\iota
, there exists a constant
C(q,T)>0
such that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{j}f|_{2}
