\displaystyle\leq 2\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\partial_{r}^{j-1}\big(f_{r}+\frac{m}{r}f\big)\Big|_{2}+C(q,T)(1+|u|_{\infty})|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{j-1}f|_{2}\quad

\displaystyle\text{for $j=1,2$},

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{k}f|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{k-2}\big(f_{r}+\frac{m}{r}f\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}+C(q,T)(1+|u|_{\infty})|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{k-1}f|_{2}\quad

\displaystyle\text{for $k=3,4$}.

Proof.

Let
q\geq 2\iota
. For brevity, we only give the proof for the highest-order estimates, and the others can be derived similarly.

First, since the following identity holds:
f_{rrrr}=r(\frac{f}{r})_{rrrr}+4(\frac{f}{r})_{rrr}
, we deduce from a direct calculation that

(7.5)

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\big(f_{r}+\frac{m}{r}f\big)_{rrr}\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi^{2q}\Big(|f_{rrrr}|^{2}+m^{2}\Big|\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{rrr}\Big|^{2}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r+2m\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi^{2q}f_{rrrr}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{rrr}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi^{2q}\Big(|f_{rrrr}|^{2}+(m^{2}+8m)\Big|\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{rrr}\Big|^{2}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r+2m\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m+1}\phi^{2q}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{rrrr}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{rrr}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi^{2q}\Big(|f_{rrrr}|^{2}\!+\!7m\Big|\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{rrr}\Big|^{2}\Big)\mathrm{d}r-\underline{\frac{2qm}{\iota}\int_{0}^{\infty}\!r^{m+1}\phi^{2q-2\iota}(\phi^{2\iota})_{r}\Big|\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{rrr}\Big|^{2}\mathrm{d}r}_{:=G_{1}}\!\!\!.

Then, for
G_{1}
, we obtain from the identity:
f_{rrr}=r(\frac{f}{r})_{rrr}+3(\frac{f}{r})_{rr}
, (
7.3
), Lemmas
4.9
and
5.5
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(7.6)

\displaystyle G_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{qm(\delta-1)}{a_{1}\delta a\iota}\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m+1}\phi^{2q-2\iota}(v-u)\Big|\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{rrr}\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq C(q)|\phi|_{\infty}^{q-2\iota}|(v,u)|_{\infty}\Big|r^{\frac{m+2}{2}}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{rrr}\Big|_{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{rrr}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(q,T)(1+|u|_{\infty})|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}f)_{r}|_{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{rrr}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(q,T)\big(1+|u|_{\infty}^{2}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}f)_{r}|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{100}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{rrr}\Big|_{2}^{2},

which, along with (
7.5
), gives

(7.7)

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}(\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}f)_{rr}|_{2}^{2}\leq 2\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\big(f_{r}+\frac{m}{r}f\big)_{rrr}\Big|_{2}^{2}+C(q,T)\big(1+|u|_{\infty}^{2}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}f)_{r}|_{2}^{2}.

Next, thanks to the identity

(7.8)

\big(\frac{f_{rr}}{r}\big)_{r}=\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{rrr}+2\Big(\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)_{r}=r\Big(\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)_{rr}+4\Big(\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)_{r},

we deduce from a direct calculation and integration by parts that

(7.9)

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\Big(\frac{1}{r}\big(f_{r}+\frac{m}{r}f\big)_{r}\Big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}=\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\Big(r\big(\frac{1}{r}(\frac{f}{r})_{r}\big)_{rr}+(m+4)\big(\frac{1}{r}(\frac{f}{r})_{r}\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi^{2q}\Big(\Big|r\Big(\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)_{rr}\Big|^{2}+(m+4)^{2}\Big|\Big(\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)_{r}\Big|^{2}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle+2(m+4)\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m+1}\phi^{2q}\Big(\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)_{rr}\Big(\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi^{2q}\Big(\Big|r\Big(\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)_{rr}\Big|^{2}+(3m+12)\Big|\Big(\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)_{r}\Big|^{2}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle-\underline{\frac{2q(m+4)}{\iota}\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m+1}\phi^{2q-2\iota}(\phi^{2\iota})_{r}\Big|\Big(\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)_{r}\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r}_{:=\widetilde{G}_{1}}.

Then, for
\widetilde{G}_{1}
, using the identity

\frac{f_{rr}}{r}=r\Big(\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)_{r}+\frac{3}{r}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{r},

repeating a calculation similar to (
7.6
), we obtain

\displaystyle\widetilde{G}_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{q(m+4)(\delta-1)}{a_{1}\delta a\iota}\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m+1}\phi^{2q-2\iota}(v-u)\Big|\Big(\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)_{r}\Big|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq C(q)|\phi|_{\infty}^{q-2\iota}|(v,u)|_{\infty}\Big|r^{\frac{m+2}{2}}\Big(\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)_{r}\Big|_{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\Big(\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)_{r}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(q,T)(1+|u|_{\infty})|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}f|_{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\Big(\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)_{r}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(q,T)\big(1+|u|_{\infty}^{2}\big)|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}f|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{100}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\Big(\frac{1}{r}\big(\frac{f}{r}\big)_{r}\Big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}.

Therefore, (
7.9
), combined with the above and (
7.8
), implies

\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\big(\frac{\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}f}{r}\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}\leq 2\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\Big(\frac{1}{r}\big(f_{r}+\frac{m}{r}f\big)_{r}\Big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}+C(q,T)\big(1+|u|_{\infty}^{2}\big)|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}f|_{2}^{2}.

which, together with (
7.7
), leads to the desired estimate.
∎

7.2.
Zeroth- and First-Order Estimates of the Velocity

The first lemma concerns the zeroth-order energy estimate for
u
.

Lemma 7.3
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u,\phi^{1-2\iota}v)(t)|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for any $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

We divide the proof into two steps.

1.
Multiplying
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
by
r^{m}u
, together with (
7.3
), gives

(7.10)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(r^{m}u^{2})_{t}+2a_{1}\delta ar^{m}\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)^{2}-2a_{1}\delta a\Big(r^{m}\underline{\phi^{2\iota}u\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)}_{:=\mathcal{B}_{11}}\Big)_{r}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\gamma-1}{2a_{1}\delta a}r^{m}\phi^{1-2\iota}(v-u)u+r^{m}(v-2u)uu_{r}.

Then we show that
r^{m}\mathrm{D}_{r}\mathcal{B}_{11}\in L^{1}(I)
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
, which allows us to apply Lemma
A.3
to obtain

(7.11)

\int_{0}^{\infty}(r^{m}\mathcal{B}_{11})_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r=0.

Indeed, based on (
4.7
), we see that, for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
,

\displaystyle(\phi^{2\iota})_{r}\in L^{\infty}(I),\quad r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u,\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u)\in L^{2}(I),\quad r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u,\mathrm{D}_{r}u)\in L^{2}(I).

Then it follows from the Hölder inequality that

\displaystyle|r^{m}\mathrm{D}_{r}\mathcal{B}_{11}|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(\big|r^{m-1}\phi^{2\iota}u\mathrm{D}_{r}u\big|_{1}+|r^{m}(\phi^{2\iota})_{r}u\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{1}+|r^{m}\phi^{2\iota}u_{r}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{1}+|r^{m}\phi^{2\iota}u\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{1}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}|(\phi^{2\iota})_{r}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}\big)<\infty.

Thus, integrating (
7.10
) over
I
, together with (
7.11
), yields

(7.12)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+2a_{1}\delta a\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{4a_{1}\delta a\iota}\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi^{1-2\iota}(v-u)u\,\mathrm{d}r+\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}(v-2u)uu_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r:=\sum_{i=2}^{3}G_{i}.

Then, for
G_{2}
–
G_{3}
, it follows from (
7.3
), Lemmas
4.9
,
5.5
, and
7.2
,
and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(7.13)

\displaystyle G_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{1-2\iota}v|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{1-2\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi^{1-2\iota}v,u)|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle G_{3}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-\iota}|(v,u)|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}u_{r}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+|u|_{\infty}^{2}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+\frac{a_{1}\delta a}{8}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}.

Substituting (
7.13
) into (
7.12
), along with Lemma
7.2
, leads to

(7.14)

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+a_{1}\delta a|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T)\big(1+|u|_{\infty}^{2}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi^{1-2\iota}v,u)|_{2}^{2}.

2.
L^{2}(I)
-estimate for
r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{1-2\iota}v
.
We multiply (
7.4
) by
r^{m}\phi^{2-4\iota}v
and use
\eqref{e2.2}_{1}
to obtain

(7.15)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(r^{m}\phi^{2-4\iota}v^{2})_{t}+\frac{\gamma-1}{2a_{1}\delta a}r^{m}\phi^{3-6\iota}v^{2}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}(r^{m}\underline{u\phi^{2-4\iota}v^{2}}_{:=\mathcal{B}_{12}})_{r}-\big(\frac{2\delta+1}{2}-\gamma\big)r^{m}\phi^{2-4\iota}v^{2}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)+\frac{\gamma-1}{2a_{1}\delta a}r^{m}\phi^{3-2\iota}vu.

Next, we need to show that
r^{m}\mathrm{D}_{r}\mathcal{B}_{12}\in L^{1}(I)
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
, which allows us to apply Lemma
A.3
to obtain

(7.16)

\int_{0}^{\infty}(r^{m}\mathcal{B}_{12})_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r=0.

Indeed, (
4.7
)–(
4.8
), (
6.12
), and Lemmas
A.1
and
B.1
imply

(\phi,u,\mathrm{D}_{r}u,v)\in L^{\infty}(I),\quad r^{\frac{m}{n^{*}}}\phi\in L^{n^{*}}(I),\quad r^{\frac{m}{n}}\psi_{r}\in L^{n}(I),\quad r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{r},u,u_{r})\in L^{2}(I)

for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
, where
n^{*}
is defined in §
3.1.3
. Then it follows from (
7.3
) and the Hölder inequality that

\displaystyle|r^{m}\mathrm{D}_{r}\mathcal{B}_{12}|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big|\big(r^{m-1}u\phi^{2-4\iota}v^{2},r^{m}u_{r}\phi^{2-4\iota}v^{2},r^{m}u\phi^{1-4\iota}\phi_{r}v^{2},r^{m}u\phi^{2-4\iota}vv_{r}\big)\big|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}\big(|\phi|_{\infty}^{2-4\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{1-4\iota}|v|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}\big(|v|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}+|\phi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{n^{*}}}\phi|_{n^{*}}|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\psi_{r}|_{n}\big)<\infty.

Thus, integrating (
7.15
) over
I
, we obtain from (
7.16
), Lemmas
4.9
and
5.5
,
and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(7.17)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{1-2\iota}v|_{2}^{2}+\frac{\gamma-1}{2a_{1}\delta a}\big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\frac{3}{2}-3\iota}v\big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\big(\frac{2\delta+1}{2}-\gamma\big)\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi^{2-4\iota}v^{2}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\,\mathrm{d}r+\frac{\gamma-1}{2a_{1}\delta a}\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi^{6-4\iota}vu\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{1-\iota}|v|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{1-2\iota}v|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{2-2\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{1-2\iota}v|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi^{1-2\iota}v,u)|_{2}^{2}+\frac{a_{1}\delta a}{8}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2},

which, together with (
7.14
), gives

(7.18)

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi^{1-2\iota}v,u)|_{2}^{2}+\frac{a_{1}\delta a}{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T)\big(1+|u|_{\infty}^{2}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi^{1-2\iota}v,u)|_{2}^{2}.

Applying the Grönwall inequality to (
7.18
), together with Lemma
6.4
, yields

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi^{1-2\iota}v,u)|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}

where, the
L^{2}(I)
-boundedness of
r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{0}^{1-2\iota}v_{0},u_{0})
follows from Lemmas
A.1
,
A.10
, and
B.1
:

(7.19)

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{0}^{1-2\iota}v_{0},u_{0})|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{0}^{1-2\iota}u_{0},\phi_{0}^{1-2\iota}\psi_{0},u_{0})|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(\|\phi_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{1-2\iota}\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\phi_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\big)\leq C_{0}.

This completes the proof.
∎

Next, we establish the following intermediate estimate for the first-order derivative of
u
.

Lemma 7.4
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{-\iota}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for any $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

First, multiplying
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
by
r^{m}\phi^{-2\iota}u_{t}
, together with
\eqref{e2.2}_{1}
and (
7.3
), gives

(7.20)

\displaystyle a_{1}\delta a\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big|\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+r^{m}\phi^{-2\iota}u_{t}^{2}-2a_{1}\delta a\Big(r^{m}\underline{\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)u_{t}}_{:=\mathcal{B}_{13}}\Big)_{r}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\gamma-1}{2a_{1}\delta a}r^{m}\phi^{1-4\iota}(v-u)u_{t}+r^{m}\phi^{-2\iota}u_{t}\Big((v-u)\big(\delta u_{r}+m(\delta-1)\frac{u}{r}\big)-uu_{r}\Big).

Then we show that
r^{m}\mathrm{D}_{r}\mathcal{B}_{13}\in L^{1}(I)
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
, which allows us to apply Lemma
A.3
to obtain

(7.21)

\int_{0}^{\infty}(r^{m}\mathcal{B}_{13})_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r=0.

To obtain this, we see from (
4.7
) that

r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\mathrm{D}_{r}u,\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u,u_{t},\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t})\in L^{2}(I)\qquad\text{for {a.e.} $t\in(0,T)$}.

Then it follows from the Hölder inequality that

\displaystyle|r^{m}\mathrm{D}_{r}\mathcal{B}_{13}|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{m-1}(\mathrm{D}_{r}u)u_{t}|_{1}+C_{0}|r^{m}(\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u)u_{t}|_{1}+C_{0}|r^{m}(\mathrm{D}_{r}u)u_{tr}|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}<\infty.

Next, integrating (
7.20
) over
I
, together with (
7.21
), we have

(7.22)

\displaystyle a_{1}\delta a\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{-\iota}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\gamma-1}{2a_{1}\delta a}\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi^{1-4\iota}(v-u)u_{t}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi^{-2\iota}u_{t}\Big((v-u)\big(\delta u_{r}+m(\delta-1)\frac{u}{r}\big)-uu_{r}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r:=\sum_{i=4}^{5}G_{i}.

For
G_{4}
–
G_{5}
, by Lemmas
4.9
,
5.5
,
and
7.3
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities, we have

(7.23)

\displaystyle G_{4}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|\phi|_{\infty}^{-\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{1-2\iota}v|_{2}+|\phi|_{\infty}^{1-3\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{-\iota}u_{t}|_{2}\leq C(T)+\frac{1}{8}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{-\iota}u_{t}|^{2}_{2},

\displaystyle G_{5}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-\iota}|(v,u)|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{-\iota}u_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+|u|_{\infty}^{2}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{8}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{-\iota}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}.

Combining (
7.22
)–(
7.23
) gives

a_{1}\delta a\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{-\iota}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T)\big(1+|u|_{\infty}^{2}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}+C(T),

which, along with Lemmas
6.4
and
7.1
, the Grönwall inequality, and the following estimate for the initial data in view of Lemma
B.1
:

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{0}|_{2}\leq C_{0}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C_{0},

yields the desired estimate.
∎

The following lemma concerns the first-order energy estimate of
u
.

Lemma 7.5
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u(t)|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for any $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

We divide the proof into two steps.

1.
Multiplying
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
by
r^{\frac{m}{2}}
and taking the
L^{2}(I)
-norm of the resulting equality, we obtain from (
7.3
), Lemmas
4.9
,
5.5
, and
7.3
–
7.4
that

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{t},uu_{r},\phi_{r},\psi\mathrm{D}_{r}u)|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{t},uu_{r})|_{2}+|\phi|_{\infty}^{-2\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi(v-u)|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(v-u)\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}|(v,u)|_{\infty}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi v|_{2}+|\phi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+|u|_{\infty}+1\big),

which, along with Lemmas
4.9
,
7.2
, and
7.4
, yields that, for any
q\in[2\iota,0]
,

(7.24)

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}\leq|\phi|_{\infty}^{q-2\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+|u|_{\infty}+1\big).

Next, it follows from (
7.24
), Lemmas
6.4
,
7.3
–
7.4
,
A.1
, and
A.4
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that, for all
t\in[0,T]
,

(7.25)

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{m+1}{2}}(\mathrm{D}_{r}u,\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u)\big|_{2}+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}(r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u)_{r}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\mathrm{D}_{r}u,\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u)|_{2}\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+|u|_{\infty}+1\big).

Finally, according to (
7.3
), Lemmas
4.9
,
5.5
, and
7.3
, we have

(7.26)

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{1-2\iota}(v-u)|_{2}\leq C_{0}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{1-2\iota}v|_{2}+|\phi|_{\infty}^{1-2\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}\big)\leq C(T).

2.
It follows from
\eqref{e2.2}_{1}
that

(7.27)

(\phi^{2\iota})_{t}+u(\phi^{2\iota})_{r}+(\delta-1)\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)=0.

Multiplying
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
by
r^{m}u_{t}
, together with
\eqref{e2.2}_{1}
, (
7.3
), and (
7.27
), gives

(7.28)

\displaystyle a_{1}\delta a\Big(r^{m}\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)^{2}\Big)_{t}+r^{m}u_{t}^{2}-2a_{1}\delta a\Big(r^{m}\underline{\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)u_{t}}_{:=\mathcal{B}_{14}}\Big)_{r}

\displaystyle=r^{m}(v-2u)u_{r}u_{t}-r^{m}\phi_{r}u_{t}+\frac{1-\delta}{2}r^{m}(v-u)u\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)^{2}

\displaystyle-a_{1}\delta(\delta-1)ar^{m}\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)^{3}.

Next, we need to show that
r^{m}\mathrm{D}_{r}\mathcal{B}_{14}\in L^{1}(I)
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
, which allows us to apply Lemma
A.3
to obtain

(7.29)

\int_{0}^{\infty}(r^{m}\mathcal{B}_{14})_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r=0.

To prove this, by (
4.7
), we have

r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u,u_{t},\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u,\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t})\in L^{2}(I)\qquad\text{for {\it a.e.} $t\in(0,T)$}.

This implies from the Hölder inequality that

\displaystyle|r^{m}\mathrm{D}_{r}\mathcal{B}_{14}|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{m-1}\phi^{2\iota}u_{t}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{1}+C_{0}|r^{m}\phi^{2\iota}u_{tr}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{1}+C_{0}|r^{m}\phi^{2\iota}u_{t}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}<\infty.

Therefore, integrating (
7.28
) over
I
, together with (
7.29
), yields

(7.30)

\displaystyle a_{1}\delta a\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}((v-2u)u_{r}-\phi_{r})u_{t}\,\mathrm{d}r+\frac{1-\delta}{2}\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}(v-u)u\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle-a_{1}\delta(\delta-1)a\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)^{3}\,\mathrm{d}r:=\sum_{i=6}^{8}G_{i}.

Then, for
G_{6}
–
G_{7}
, it follows from (
7.26
), Lemmas
4.9
and
5.5
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(7.31)

\displaystyle G_{6}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|\phi|_{\infty}^{-\iota}|(u,v)|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}u_{r}|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+|u|_{\infty}^{2}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}u_{r}|_{2}^{2}+C(T)+\frac{1}{8}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle G_{7}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-2\iota}|(v,u)|_{\infty}|u|_{\infty}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T)\big(1+|u|_{\infty}^{2}\big)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}.

To estimate
G_{8}
, we see from (
7.25
) that

(7.32)

\displaystyle G_{8}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big|\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+|u|_{\infty}+1\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big|\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+C(T)\big(1+|u|_{\infty}^{2}\big)+\frac{1}{8}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}.

Collecting (
7.30
)–(
7.32
), we obtain
from Lemmas
7.2
–
7.3
that

(7.33)

\displaystyle a_{1}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big|\phi^{\iota}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)+C(T)\Big(1+|u|_{\infty}^{2}+\Big|\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\Big)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(T)\big(1+|u|_{\infty}^{2}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}u_{r}|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(1+|u|_{\infty}^{2}+\Big|\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\Big)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}+C(T)\big(1+|u|_{\infty}^{4}\big),

which, along with Lemmas
6.4
and
7.2
and the Grönwall inequality, yields

(7.34)

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u(t)|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{0}^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{0}|_{2}^{2}+C(T)\leq C(T).

Here, for the estimate of the initial data, it follows from the initial compatibility condition
\eqref{th78zxq}_{1}
and Lemma
B.1
that

(7.35)

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{0}^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{0}|_{2}\leq C_{0}\|\phi_{0}^{\iota}\nabla\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C_{0}\|\mathcal{G}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C_{0}.

This completes the proof.
∎

7.3.
Second-Order Estimates of the Velocity

Lemma 7.6
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+\int_{0}^{t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for any $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

We divide the proof into three steps.

1.
Multiplying
\eqref{e2.2}_{1}
by
r^{\frac{m}{2}}
and taking the
L^{2}(I)
-norm of the resulting equation, we obtain from (
7.24
), (
7.26
), and Lemmas
4.9
and
7.4
that

(7.36)

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u\phi_{r}|_{2}+(\gamma-1)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}\leq C(T)(|u|_{\infty}+1).

2.
Applying
\partial_{t}
to
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
, together with
\eqref{e2.2}_{3}
, (
7.3
), and (
7.27
), gives

(7.37)

\displaystyle u_{tt}

\displaystyle=-(u_{t}u_{r}+uu_{tr})-\phi_{tr}+2a_{1}\delta a\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)_{r}+(1-\delta)u(v-u)\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}

\displaystyle-2a_{1}\delta a\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\big((2\delta-1)u_{r}+2m(\delta-1)\frac{u}{r}\big)-2a_{1}\psi_{r}u\big(\delta u_{r}+m(\delta-1)\frac{u}{r}\big)

\displaystyle-(v-u)\big(\delta u_{r}+m(\delta-1)\frac{u}{r}\big)^{2}+(v-u)\big(\delta u_{tr}+m(\delta-1)\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big).

Then, multiplying the (
7.37
) by
r^{m}u_{t}
, we obtain from (
7.3
) that

(7.38)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(r^{m}u_{t}^{2})_{t}+2a_{1}\delta ar^{m}\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle=\Big(r^{m}\big(\underline{2a_{1}\delta a\phi^{2\iota}u_{t}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)-u_{t}\phi_{t}-2a_{1}\psi u\big(\delta u_{r}+m(\delta-1)\frac{u}{r}\big)u_{t}\big)}_{:=\mathcal{B}_{15}}\Big)_{r}

\displaystyle-r^{m}(u_{t}u_{r}+uu_{tr})u_{t}+r^{m}\phi_{t}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)

\displaystyle-2a_{1}\delta ar^{m}\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\big((2\delta-1)u_{r}+2m(\delta-1)\frac{u}{r}\big)u_{t}

\displaystyle+r^{m}(v-u)\big(\delta u_{r}+m(\delta-1)\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big(u\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)-(\delta-1)\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)u_{t}\Big)

\displaystyle+r^{m}(v-u)(uu_{rr}+u_{tr})u_{t}.

Next, we need to show that
r^{m}\mathrm{D}_{r}\mathcal{B}_{15}\in L^{1}(I)
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
, which allows us to apply Lemma
A.3
to obtain

(7.39)

\int_{0}^{\infty}(r^{m}\mathcal{B}_{15})_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r=0.

By (
4.7
)–(
4.8
) and Lemmas
A.1
and
B.1
, we obtain that, for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
,

\displaystyle(\psi,u,\mathrm{D}_{r}u)\in L^{\infty}(I),\qquad r^{\frac{m}{n^{*}}}u\in L^{n^{*}}(I),\qquad r^{\frac{m}{n}}\psi_{r}\in L^{n}(I),

\displaystyle r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\phi_{t},\mathrm{D}_{r}u,u_{t},\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u,\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t},\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t},\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t}\big)\in L^{2}(I).

Then it follows from the Hölder inequality that

\displaystyle|r^{m}\mathrm{D}_{r}\mathcal{B}_{15}|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big|r^{m-1}u_{t}(\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t},\phi_{t},\psi u\mathrm{D}_{r}u)\big|_{1}+C_{0}\big|r^{m}u_{tr}(\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t},\phi_{t},\psi u\mathrm{D}_{r}u)\big|_{1}

\displaystyle+C_{0}\big|r^{m}u_{t}(\psi\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t},\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t},\phi_{tr},\psi_{r}u\mathrm{D}_{r}u,\psi u_{r}\mathrm{D}_{r}u,\psi u\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u)\big|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{t}|_{2}+|\psi|_{\infty}|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}\big(|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t},\phi_{tr})|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\psi_{r}|_{n}|r^{\frac{m}{n^{*}}}u|_{n^{*}}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}\big)

\displaystyle+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}|\psi|_{\infty}\big(|u_{r}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}+|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}\big)<\infty.

Now, integrating (
7.38
) over
I
, together with (
7.39
), yields

(7.40)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|^{2}_{2}+2a_{1}\delta a\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}(u_{t}u_{r}+uu_{tr})u_{t}\,\mathrm{d}r+\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi_{t}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle-2a_{1}\delta a\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\big((2\delta-1)u_{r}+2m(\delta-1)\frac{u}{r}\big)u_{t}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}(v-u)\big(\delta u_{r}+m(\delta-1)\frac{u}{r}\big)\Big(u\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)-(\delta-1)\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)u_{t}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}(v-u)(uu_{rr}+u_{tr})u_{t}\,\mathrm{d}r:=\sum_{i=9}^{13}G_{i}.

3.
For
G_{9}
–
G_{13}
, it follows from (
7.24
)–(
7.25
), (
7.36
), Lemmas
4.9
,
6.4
,
7.2
,
7.5
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} G_{9}&\leq|\phi|_{\infty}^{-\iota}|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}u_{tr}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+\big(|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}u_{t}|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}u_{t}|_{2}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}\\
&\leq C(T)|u|_{\infty}|\phi^{\iota}r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tr}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+|\phi|_{\infty}^{-\iota}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{2-m}{2}}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\phi^{\iota}u_{t}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}\\
&\quad+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{-\frac{m}{2}}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}\\
&\leq C(T)\big(|(u,r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r})|_{\infty}^{2}+1\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+\frac{a_{1}\delta a}{80}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}\\
&\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+|u|_{\infty}^{2}+1\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+\frac{a_{1}\delta a}{80}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2},\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} G_{10}&\leq|\phi|_{\infty}^{-\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{t}|_{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}\leq C(T)(|u|_{\infty}+1)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}\\
&\leq C(T)\big(|u|_{\infty}^{2}+1\big)+\frac{a_{1}\delta a}{80}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2},\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} G_{11}&\leq C_{0}|\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}\\
&\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+|u|_{\infty}^{2}+1\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+C(T)|\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2},\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} G_{12}&\leq|(v,u)|_{\infty}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-2\iota}|u|_{\infty}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u\Big|_{2}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}\\
&\quad+|(v,u)|_{\infty}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-\iota}|\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}\\
&\leq C(T)\big(1+|u|_{\infty}^{2}\big)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}\\
&\quad+C(T)(1+|u|_{\infty})|\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}\big(1+|u|_{\infty}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}\big)\\
&\leq C(T)\big(|u|_{\infty}^{4}+|\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}+1\big)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+1\big)+\frac{a_{1}\delta a}{80}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2},\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} G_{13}&\leq C_{0}|(v,u)|_{\infty}\big(|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{rr}|_{2}+|\phi|_{\infty}^{-\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}u_{tr}|_{2}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}\\
&\leq C(T)\big((|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+|u|_{\infty}^{2}+1)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+|u|_{\infty}^{4}+1\big)+\frac{a_{1}\delta a}{80}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}.\end{aligned}

Substituting the above into (
7.40
), along with Lemma
7.2
, gives

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|^{2}_{2}+a_{1}\delta a|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+|u|_{\infty}^{4}+|\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}+1\big)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+1\big).

Integrating over
(\tau,t)(\tau\in(0,t))
yields

(7.41)

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}(t)|_{2}^{2}+a_{1}\delta a\int_{\tau}^{t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}(\tau)|_{2}^{2}+C(T)\int_{\tau}^{t}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+|u|_{\infty}^{4}+|\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{2}+1\big)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+1\big)\,\mathrm{d}s.

For the
L^{2}(I)
-boundedness of
r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}(\tau)

on the right-hand side of (
7.41
), we multiply
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
by
r^{\frac{m}{2}}
and then take the
L^{2}(I)
-norm of the resulting equality to obtain

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}(\tau)|_{2}\leq C_{0}\big(|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}+\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}+|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}\big)(\tau).

This, along with the time-continuity of
(\phi,u,\psi)
, the initial compatibility condition
\eqref{th78zxq}_{3}
, and
Lemmas
A.1
,
A.10
, and
B.1
, yields

\displaystyle\limsup_{\tau\to 0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}(\tau)|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\phi_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\phi_{0}^{2\iota}L\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\boldsymbol{\psi}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{H^{2}}+\|\boldsymbol{\psi}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big)\|\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{H^{2}}+C_{0}\|\boldsymbol{g}_{*}\|_{L^{2}}+C_{0}\|\nabla\phi_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C_{0}.

where we have also used the following fact, since
\boldsymbol{u}_{0}
is spherically symmetric:

(7.42)

L\boldsymbol{u}_{0}=-a_{1}\Delta\boldsymbol{u}_{0}-(2\delta-1)a_{1}\nabla\diver\boldsymbol{u}_{0}=-2\delta a_{1}\nabla\diver\boldsymbol{u}_{0}.

Hence, based on the above discussions, letting
\tau\to 0
in (
7.41
), combined with Lemmas
6.4
and
7.5
and the Grönwall inequality, leads to the desired estimate.
∎

With the help of Lemma
7.6
, we can also obtain the following estimates:

Lemma 7.7
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that, for any
t\in[0,T]
and
q\in[2\iota,0]
,

|(u,r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r})(t)|_{\infty}+\delta_{3n}|r^{\frac{m}{6}}(\phi,u,\mathrm{D}_{r}u)(t)|_{6}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{r},\phi_{t},\phi^{q}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u)(t)|_{2}+\int_{0}^{t}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T).

Proof.

First, it follows from (
7.24
), Lemmas
4.9
,
7.1
–
7.3
,
7.6
,
A.2
, and
B.1
that

\displaystyle|u|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\|\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C_{0}\|\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{4-n}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{n}{4}}\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}^{\frac{4-n}{4}}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{\frac{n}{4}}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+|u|_{\infty}+1\big)^{\frac{n}{4}}\leq C(T)\big(|u|_{\infty}^{\frac{n}{4}}+1\big),

which, along with the Young inequality, leads to

|u(t)|_{\infty}\leq C(T).

Clearly, the above, together with (
7.24
)–(
7.26
) and (
7.36
) and Lemma
7.6
, also gives that, for all
t\in[0,T]
and
q\in[2\iota,0]
,

(7.43)

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{r}(t)|_{\infty}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{r},\phi_{t})(t)|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u(t)|_{2}\leq C(T).

Next, multiplying
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
by
r^{\frac{m}{4}}
and then taking the
L^{4}(I)
-norm of the resulting equality, we obtain from (
7.3
), (
7.43
), and Lemmas
4.9
,
7.1
,
7.3
,
7.6
,
A.2
, and
B.1
that

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{4}}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big|_{4}\leq C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-2\iota}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{4}}\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big|_{4}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{4}}u_{t}|_{4}+|(u,v)|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{4}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{4}+|r^{\frac{m}{4}}\phi^{1-2\iota}(v-u)|_{4}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\|(\boldsymbol{u}_{t},\nabla\boldsymbol{u})\|_{L^{4}}+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1-2\iota}{2}}|(u,v)|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{1-2\iota}v|_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}+|\phi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1-2\iota}{2}}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u|_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\|(\boldsymbol{u}_{t},\nabla\boldsymbol{u})\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{4-n}{4}}\|(\nabla\boldsymbol{u}_{t},\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{u})\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{n}{4}}+1\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{t},\mathrm{D}_{r}u)|_{2}^{\frac{4-n}{4}}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t},\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u)|_{2}^{\frac{n}{4}}+1\big)\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}^{\frac{n}{4}}+1\big),

which, together with Lemmas
7.4
,
A.2
, and
B.1
, and the Young inequality, yields

(7.44)

\displaystyle|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C_{0}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{4-n}{n+4}}\|\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{2n}{n+4}}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{\frac{4-n}{n+4}}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}^{\frac{n^{2}}{2(n+4)}}+1\big)\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}+1\big).

This, along with Lemma
7.6
, yields the
L^{2}([0,T];L^{\infty})
estimate for
\mathrm{D}_{r}u
.

Finally, when
n=3
, by (
7.43
) and Lemmas
A.1
and
B.1
, we have

(7.45)

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{6}}(\phi,u,\mathrm{D}_{r}u)|_{6}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\|(\phi,\boldsymbol{u},\nabla\boldsymbol{u})\|_{L^{6}}\leq C_{0}\|(\nabla\phi,\nabla\boldsymbol{u},\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{u})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{r},\mathrm{D}_{r}u,\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u)|_{2}\leq C(T).

This completes the proof.
∎

We now show the first-order estimates for
(\psi,v)
.

Lemma 7.8
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

|\psi(t)|_{\infty}+|r^{\frac{m}{n}}(\mathrm{D}_{r}v,\mathrm{D}_{r}\psi)(t)|_{n}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{t}(t)|_{2}\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for any $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

First, it follows from (
7.3
) and Lemmas
5.5
and
7.7
that

|\psi(t)|_{\infty}\leq C_{0}|(v,u)(t)|_{\infty}\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Next, define the effective velocity
\boldsymbol{v}
in coordinates
(t,\boldsymbol{x})
as

(7.46)

\boldsymbol{v}=\boldsymbol{u}+\frac{2a_{1}\delta a}{\delta-1}\nabla\phi^{2\iota}.

Then, from
\eqref{eq:cccq}_{2}
and (
3.7
), we can obtain its equation:

(7.47)

\boldsymbol{v}_{t}+\boldsymbol{u}\cdot\nabla\boldsymbol{v}+\frac{\gamma-1}{2a_{1}\delta a}\phi^{1-2\iota}(\boldsymbol{v}-\boldsymbol{u})=\boldsymbol{0}.

Applying
\mathrm{div}
to (
7.47
) leads to the following equation in the sense of distributions:

(7.48)

\displaystyle(\diver\boldsymbol{v})_{t}+\diver\big(\boldsymbol{u}(\diver\boldsymbol{v})\big)

\displaystyle=(\diver\boldsymbol{u})(\diver\boldsymbol{v})-\nabla\boldsymbol{u}^{\top}:\nabla\boldsymbol{v}

\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma-\delta}{2a_{1}\delta a}\phi^{-2\iota}\nabla\phi\cdot(\boldsymbol{v}-\boldsymbol{u})-\frac{\gamma-1}{2a_{1}\delta a}\phi^{1-2\iota}(\diver\boldsymbol{v}-\diver\boldsymbol{u}).

Using (
7.46
) and Lemmas
3.1
and
A.1
, we obtain

(7.49)

\diver\boldsymbol{v}\in L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{n}(\mathbb{R}^{n})),\qquad\boldsymbol{u}\in L^{1}([0,T];W^{1,\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{n})),

and the right-hand side of (
7.48
) belongs to
L^{1}([0,T];L^{n}(\mathbb{R}^{n}))
.
Thus, it follows from Lemma
A.11
that, for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
,

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\diver\boldsymbol{v}\|_{L^{n}}^{n}+\frac{\gamma-1}{a_{1}\delta a}\big\|\phi^{\frac{1-2\iota}{n}}\diver\boldsymbol{v}\big\|_{L^{n}}^{n}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}(\diver\boldsymbol{u})|\diver\boldsymbol{v}|^{n}\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x}-n\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}(\nabla\boldsymbol{u}^{\top}:\nabla\boldsymbol{v})|\diver\boldsymbol{v}|^{n-2}\diver\boldsymbol{v}\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Big(-\frac{n(\gamma-\delta)}{2a_{1}\delta a}\phi^{-2\iota}\nabla\phi\cdot(\boldsymbol{v}-\boldsymbol{u})+\frac{n(\gamma-1)}{2a_{1}\delta a}\phi^{1-2\iota}(\diver\boldsymbol{u})\Big)|\diver\boldsymbol{v}|^{n-2}\diver\boldsymbol{v}\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x}.

This, along with the spherical coordinate transformation, yields that,
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
,

(7.50)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)\Big|_{n}^{n}+\frac{\gamma-1}{a_{1}\delta a}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\phi^{\frac{1-2\iota}{n}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)\Big|_{n}^{n}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\Big|^{n}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle-n\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\Big(u_{r}v_{r}+\frac{m}{r^{2}}uv\Big)\Big|v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\Big|^{n-2}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\Big(-\frac{n(\gamma-\delta)}{2a_{1}\delta a}\phi^{-2\iota}\phi_{r}(v-u)+\frac{n(\gamma-1)}{2a_{1}\delta a}\phi^{1-2\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big)

\displaystyle\times\Big|v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\Big|^{n-2}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)\,\mathrm{d}r.

We now continue to estimate the right-hand side of (
7.50
). To achieve this, we first present some higher-order estimates for
\phi
. It follows from (
7.1
), (
7.3
), Lemmas
4.9
and
7.7
–
7.8
that

(7.51)

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}\phi_{r}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}(\phi^{1-2\iota}\psi)|_{2}\leq C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-2\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{r}\psi,\phi\mathrm{D}_{r}\psi)|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-2\iota}|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-2\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{n^{*}}}\phi|_{n^{*}}|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\mathrm{D}_{r}v|_{n}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-2\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{n^{*}}}\phi|_{n^{*}}|r^{\frac{m}{n^{*}}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{n^{*}}^{\frac{2-n}{2}}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{\frac{4-n}{2}}\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\mathrm{D}_{r}v|_{n}+1\big),

which, along with Lemmas
A.1
,
B.1
, and
C.2
, leads to

(7.52)

|r^{\frac{m}{n^{*}}}\phi_{r}|_{n^{*}}\leq C_{0}\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{n^{*}}}\leq C_{0}\|\nabla^{2}\phi\|_{L^{2}}\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}\phi_{r}|_{2}\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\mathrm{D}_{r}v|_{n}+1\big).

Hence, by (
7.51
)–(
7.52
), Lemmas
4.9
,
7.4
, and
7.7
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)\Big|_{n}^{n}+\frac{\gamma-1}{a_{1}\delta a}\Big|(r^{m}\phi^{1-2\iota})^{\frac{1}{n}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)\Big|_{n}^{n}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\mathrm{D}_{r}v|_{n}^{n}+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-2\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{n^{*}}}\phi_{r}|_{n^{*}}^{\frac{n-2}{2}}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}^{\frac{4-n}{2}}|(v,u)|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}v|_{n}^{n-1}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{1-2\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{n^{*}}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{n^{*}}^{\frac{n-2}{2}}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}^{\frac{4-n}{2}}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}v|_{n}^{n-1}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}^{2}+1\big)|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\mathrm{D}_{r}v|_{n}^{n}+C(T),

which, along with Lemmas
7.1
,
7.7
,
A.1
, and
B.1
, and the Grönwall inequality, gives

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\mathrm{D}_{r}v(t)|_{n}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\mathrm{D}_{r}v_{0}|_{n}+1\big)\leq C(T)\big(\big|r^{\frac{m}{n}}(\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{0},\mathrm{D}_{r}(\rho_{0}^{\delta-1})_{r})\big|_{n}+1\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\|(\nabla\boldsymbol{u}_{0},\nabla^{2}\rho_{0}^{\delta-1})\|_{L^{n}}+1\big)\leq C(T).

This, together with (
7.3
) and Lemma
7.4
, yields

|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\mathrm{D}_{r}\psi(t)|_{n}\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{n}}(\mathrm{D}_{r}v,\mathrm{D}_{r}u)(t)|_{n}\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Finally, multiplying
\eqref{e2.2}_{3}
by
r^{\frac{m}{2}}
and then taking the
L^{2}(I)
-norm of the resulting equality, we obtain from the above and Lemmas
7.4
and
7.7
that

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u\psi_{r}|_{2}+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|r^{\frac{m}{n^{*}}}u|_{n^{*}}|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\psi_{r}|_{n}+C_{0}|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}+C(T)\leq C(T).

This completes the proof.
∎

The following lemma provides the higher-order estimates of
(\phi,u)
.

Lemma 7.9
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that, for any
q\in[2\iota,0]
,

|r^{\frac{m}{n^{*}}}\phi_{r}(t)|_{n^{*}}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\mathrm{D}_{r}\phi_{r},\phi_{tr})(t)|_{2}+\int_{0}^{t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{3}u|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for any $t\in[0,T]$},

where
n^{*}
is defined as in
§
3.1.3
.

Proof.

We divide the proof into two steps.

1. Estimates on
\phi
.
First, by (
7.51
)–(
7.52
) and Lemma
7.8
, we have

|r^{\frac{m}{n^{*}}}\phi_{r}(t)|_{n^{*}}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}\phi_{r}(t)|_{2}\leq C(T).

Next, for the
L^{2}(I)
-estimate of
r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{tr}
, it follows from the above, (
7.1
), and Lemmas
4.9
and
7.7
–
7.8
that

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{tr}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi^{1-2\iota}\psi)_{t}|_{2}\leq C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-2\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{t}\psi,\phi\psi_{t})|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-2\iota}(|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{t}|_{2}+|\phi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{t}|_{2})\leq C(T).

2. Estimates on
u
.
Applying
r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}
on both sides of
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
and taking
L^{2}(I)
-norm of the resulting equality,
we obtain from Lemmas
4.9
,
7.4
–
7.5
,
and
7.7
–
7.8
that

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}\big(\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(\big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t},|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|^{2},u\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u\big)\big|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{-2\iota}\phi_{r}\psi|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle+C_{0}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{1-2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}\psi|_{2}+\big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(|\mathrm{D}_{r}\psi||\mathrm{D}_{r}u|,\psi\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u\big)\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|\phi|_{\infty}^{-\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}+|u_{r}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}+|(u,\psi)|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-2\iota}|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-2\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{n^{*}}}\phi|_{n^{*}}|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\mathrm{D}_{r}\psi|_{n}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{n^{*}}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{n^{*}}|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\mathrm{D}_{r}\psi|_{n}\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}+|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}+1\big),

which, along with (
7.1
) and Lemmas
7.7
–
7.8
, implies

(7.53)

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}\Big(\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}+C_{0}|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|\phi^{\iota}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}+|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}+1\big).

Thus, we obtain from (
7.53
) and Lemmas
7.2
and
7.6
–
7.7
that

(7.54)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{3}u|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\int_{0}^{t}\big(|\phi^{\iota}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}^{2}+1\big)\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T).

Finally, using Lemma
4.9
again, we arrive at the desired estimates.
∎

7.4.
Third-Order Energy Estimates of the Velocity

Lemma 7.10
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

\delta_{3n}|r^{\frac{m}{6}}u_{t}(t)|_{6}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}(t)|_{2}+\int_{0}^{t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|^{2}_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for any $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

We divide the proof into two steps.

1.
Applying
r^{m}u_{tt}
to
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
, we obtain

(7.55)

a_{1}\delta a\Big(r^{m}\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)^{2}\Big)_{t}+r^{m}u_{tt}^{2}=2a_{1}\delta a\Big(r^{m}\underline{\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)u_{tt}}_{:=\mathcal{B}_{16}}\Big)_{r}+G_{14},

where

\displaystyle G_{14}

\displaystyle=-r^{m}(u_{t}u_{r}+uu_{tr}+\phi_{tr})+2a_{1}\delta a\iota r^{m}(\log\phi)_{t}\phi^{2\iota}\Big(2\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}u_{tt}+\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+2a_{1}r^{m}\psi_{t}\big(\delta u_{r}+m(\delta-1)\frac{u}{r}\big)u_{tt}+2a_{1}r^{m}\psi\big(\delta u_{tr}+m(\delta-1)\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)u_{tt}.

2.
We show that
r^{m}\mathrm{D}_{r}\mathcal{B}_{16}\in L^{1}(I)
for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
, so that Lemma
A.3
implies

(7.56)

\int_{0}^{\infty}(r^{m}\mathcal{B}_{16})_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r=0.

Indeed, by (
4.7
)–(
4.8
), we see that, for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
,

(\psi,u,\mathrm{D}_{r}u)\in L^{\infty}(I),\qquad r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{t},\mathrm{D}_{r}u,u_{t},\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u,\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t},\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t},\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t})\in L^{2}(I).

Then it follows from the Hölder inequality that

\displaystyle|r^{m}\mathrm{D}_{r}\mathcal{B}_{16}|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(\big|r^{m-1}\phi^{2\iota}(\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t})u_{tt}\big|_{1}+\big|r^{m}(\psi(\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t})u_{tt},\phi^{2\iota}(\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t})u_{tt},,\phi^{2\iota}(\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t})u_{ttr})\big|_{1}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}\big(|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t}|_{2}\big)<\infty,

which thus leads to (
7.56
).

3.
To estimate
G_{14}
, we first see from from (
7.1
) and
\eqref{e2.2}_{1}
that

(7.57)

(\log\phi)_{t}+\frac{\gamma-1}{\delta a}u\phi^{-2\iota}\psi+(\gamma-1)\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)=0,

which, along with Lemmas
4.9
and
7.7
–
7.8
, yields

(7.58)

|(\log\phi)_{t}|_{\infty}\leq C_{0}\big(|u|_{\infty}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-2\iota}|\psi|_{\infty}+|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}\big)\leq C(T)\big(|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}+1\big).

Next, by (
7.58
), Lemmas
4.9
and
7.6
–
7.9
,
and the Hölder and Young inequalities, we have

(7.59)

\displaystyle|G_{14}|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{t},\psi_{t})|_{2}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}+|\phi|_{\infty}^{-\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}|(u,\psi)|_{\infty}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{tr}|_{2}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|(\log\phi)_{t}|_{\infty}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}^{2}+1\big)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+1\big)+\frac{1}{8}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}^{2}.

Hence, integrating (
7.55
) over
[\tau,t]\times I
with
\tau\in(0,t)
, along with (
7.56
)–(
7.59
), and Lemmas
7.2
and
7.6
–
7.7
, implies

(7.60)

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+\int_{\tau}^{t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|^{2}_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}(\tau)|^{2}_{2}+1\big)+C(T)\int_{\tau}^{t}\big(|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}^{2}+1\big)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+1\big)\,\mathrm{d}s.

For the
L^{2}(I)
-boundedness of
r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}(\tau)

on the right-hand side of (
7.60
), we apply
r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}
to
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
and take the
L^{2}(I)
-norm of the resulting equality. Then it follows from (
7.1
), Lemmas
4.9
and
7.6
–
7.8
, and the Hölder inequality that, for
\tau\in(0,t)
,

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2}+|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}+|\phi|_{\infty}^{1-\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}\psi|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi|_{2}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-3\iota}|\psi|_{\infty}^{2}+C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}\Big(\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\mathrm{D}_{r}\psi|_{n}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{n^{*}}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{n^{*}}+C_{0}|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}+1\big)+C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}\Big(\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\mathrm{D}_{r}\psi|_{n}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{n^{*}}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big|_{n^{*}},

which, along with the time-continuity of
(\phi,u,\psi)
, (
7.42
), the initial compatibility condition (
3.10
), and
Lemmas
A.1
,
A.10
, and
B.1
, yields

\displaystyle\limsup_{\tau\to 0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}(\tau)|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\|\nabla\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\!+\!\|\phi_{0}^{\iota}\nabla(\phi_{0}^{2\iota}L\boldsymbol{u}_{0})\|_{L^{2}}\!+\!\|\nabla\boldsymbol{\psi}_{0}\|_{L^{n}}\|\phi_{0}^{\iota}\diver\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{n^{*}}}\!+1\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\|\mathcal{G}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\phi_{0}^{\iota}\diver\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{H^{2}}+1\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\|\phi_{0}^{\iota}\nabla\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\phi_{0}^{\iota}\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{4}}+\|\phi_{0}^{\iota}L\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle+C(T)\|\phi_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{-\iota}\big(\|\nabla^{2}(\phi_{0}^{\iota}\diver\boldsymbol{u}_{0})\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\boldsymbol{\psi}_{0}\|_{L^{n}}\|\diver\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{n^{*}}}\big)

\displaystyle+C(T)\|\phi_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{-\iota}\big(\|\phi_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{-2\iota}\|\boldsymbol{\psi}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\diver\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\phi_{0}^{2\iota}L\boldsymbol{u}_{0}\|_{L^{2}}+1\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\|(\mathcal{G}_{1},\mathcal{G}_{2},\boldsymbol{g}_{*})\|_{L^{2}}+1\big)\leq C(T).

Consequently, based on the above discussions, letting
\tau\to 0
in (
7.60
), together with Lemma
7.7
and the Grönwall inequality, yields

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+\int_{0}^{t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|^{2}_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Finally, if
n=3
, it follows from the above and Lemmas
4.9
,
7.6
,
A.1
, and
B.1
that

|r^{\frac{m}{6}}u_{t}|_{6}\leq C_{0}\|\boldsymbol{u}_{t}\|_{L^{6}}\leq C_{0}\|\boldsymbol{u}_{t}\|_{H^{1}}\leq C_{0}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+|\phi|_{\infty}^{-\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}\big)\leq C(T).

This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 7.11
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that, for any
q\in[2\iota,0]
and
t\in[0,T]
,

|\mathrm{D}_{r}u(t)|_{\infty}\!+\!\delta_{3n}|r^{\frac{m}{6}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u(t)|_{6}\!+\!|r^{\frac{m}{2n}}(\phi^{\iota})_{r}(t)|_{2n}\!+\!|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{3}u(t)|_{2}\!+\!\int_{0}^{t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T).

Proof.

First, it follows from (
7.44
) and Lemmas
4.9
and
7.10
,

(7.61)

|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}\leq C(T)\big(|\phi|_{\infty}^{-\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}+1\big)\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$},

which, along with (
7.53
), and Lemmas
7.2
,
7.7
, and
7.10
, yields that, for any
q\in[2\iota,0]
,

(7.62)

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{3}u|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|\phi|_{\infty}^{q-2\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{3}u|_{2}\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}+|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}+1\big)\leq C(T).

Next, applying
r^{\frac{m}{2}}\partial_{t}
to
(\ref{e2.2})_{2}
and taking
L^{2}(I)
-norm of the resulting equality, along with (
7.58
), (
7.61
), and Lemmas
7.2
and
7.6
–
7.10
, we have

(7.63)

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{tr}\Big|_{2}+1\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{tt},(uu_{r})_{t},\phi_{tr},(\psi\mathrm{D}_{r}u)_{t},(\phi^{2\iota})_{t}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u\big)\big|_{2}+1\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{tt},\phi_{tr})|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{t},\psi_{t})|_{2}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}|(u,\psi)|_{\infty}\big)

\displaystyle+C(T)\big(|(\log\phi)_{t}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}+1\big)\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}+1\big).

This, combined with Lemmas
4.9
and
7.10
, yields the
L^{2}([0,T];L^{2})
-estimates of
r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t}
.

To obtain
L^{2n}(I)
-estimate for
r^{\frac{m}{2n}}(\phi^{\iota})_{r}
, we first see from (
7.62
) and Lemmas
4.9
,
7.7
–
7.8
,
A.1
, and
B.1
that

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2n}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2n}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\|\phi^{\iota}\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{2n}}\leq C_{0}\|(\phi^{\iota}\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{u},\phi^{-\iota}|\boldsymbol{\psi}||\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{u}|,\phi^{\iota}\nabla^{3}\boldsymbol{u})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}(\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u,\mathrm{D}_{r}^{3}u)|_{2}+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-\iota}|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}\leq C(T).

Then applying
r^{m}|(\phi^{\iota})_{r}|^{2n-2}(\phi^{\iota})_{r}\partial_{r}
to
\eqref{e2.2}_{1}\times\phi^{\iota-1}
and then integrating over
I
, together with the above, (
7.61
), and the Hölder and Young inequalities, yield

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|r^{\frac{m}{2n}}(\phi^{\iota})_{r}|_{2n}^{2n}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2n}}(\phi^{\iota})_{r}|_{2n}^{2n}+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2n}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{2n}|r^{\frac{m}{2n}}(\phi^{\iota})_{r}|_{2n}^{2n-1}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2n}}(\phi^{\iota})_{r}|_{2n}^{2n}+1\big),

which, along with the Grönwall inequality, implies the desired estimate.

Finally, if
n=3
, it follows from (
7.62
) and Lemmas
7.7
,
A.1
, and
B.1
that

|r^{\frac{m}{6}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{6}\leq C_{0}\|\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{6}}\leq C_{0}\|\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{u}\|_{H^{1}}\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u,\mathrm{D}_{r}^{3}u)|_{2}\leq C(T).

∎

7.5.
The Highest-Order Estimates of
(\phi,\boldsymbol{\psi})
.

Now, we focus on the
D^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
-estimates for
\boldsymbol{\psi}
and
D^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
-estimates for
\phi
.

Lemma 7.12
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}\psi,\mathrm{D}_{r}\psi_{t})(t)|_{2}\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for any $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

We divide the proof into three steps.

1.
It follows from Lemmas
7.8
,
A.1
, and
A.4
, and the Hölder inequality that

(7.64)

\displaystyle|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r\mathrm{D}_{r}v|_{\infty}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}\mathrm{D}_{r}v|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{3}{2}}(\mathrm{D}_{r}v,\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}v)|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}(\mathrm{D}_{r}v,\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}v)|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{3}{2}-\frac{m}{n}}|_{n^{*}}+|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{-\frac{m}{n}}|_{n^{*}}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\mathrm{D}_{r}v|_{n}+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}v|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}v|_{2}^{2}+1\big).

2.

Applying
\partial_{l}
(
l=1,\cdots\!,n)
to both sides of (
7.48
) leads to the following equation in the sense of distributions:

(7.65)

\displaystyle(\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{v})_{t}+\diver\big(\boldsymbol{u}(\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{v})\big)

\displaystyle=(\diver\boldsymbol{u})(\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{v})-\partial_{l}\boldsymbol{u}\cdot\nabla(\diver\boldsymbol{v})-\partial_{l}(\nabla\boldsymbol{u}^{\top}:\nabla\boldsymbol{v})

\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma-\delta}{2a_{1}\delta a}\big(\frac{1-\delta}{\delta a}\phi^{-4\iota}\psi_{l}\nabla\phi+\phi^{-2\iota}\nabla(\partial_{l}\phi)\big)\cdot(\boldsymbol{v}-\boldsymbol{u})-\frac{\gamma-\delta}{2a_{1}\delta a}\phi^{-2\iota}\nabla\phi\cdot(\partial_{l}\boldsymbol{v}-\partial_{l}\boldsymbol{u})

\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma-\delta}{2a_{1}\delta a}\phi^{-2\iota}\partial_{l}\phi(\diver\boldsymbol{v}-\diver\boldsymbol{u})-\frac{\gamma-1}{2a_{1}\delta a}\phi^{1-2\iota}(\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{v}-\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{u}).

It can be checked due to
\boldsymbol{v}=\boldsymbol{u}+2a_{1}\nabla\log\rho
, and Lemmas
3.1
and
A.1
that

\nabla\diver\boldsymbol{v}\in L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{n})),\quad\boldsymbol{u}\in L^{1}([0,T];W^{1,\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{n})),

and the right-hand side of (
7.65
) belongs to
L^{1}([0,T];L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{n}))
.
Then it follows from Lemma
A.11
that, for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
,

(7.66)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{v}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\gamma-1}{a_{1}\delta a}\big\|\phi^{\frac{1}{2}-\iota}\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{v}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\big((\diver\boldsymbol{u})(\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{v})-2(\partial_{l}\boldsymbol{u}\cdot\nabla\diver\boldsymbol{v})-2\partial_{l}(\nabla\boldsymbol{u}^{\top}:\nabla\boldsymbol{v})\big)(\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{v})\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x}

\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma-\delta}{a_{1}\delta a}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\big(\frac{1-\delta}{\delta a}\phi^{-4\iota}\psi_{l}\nabla\phi+\phi^{-2\iota}\nabla(\partial_{l}\phi)\big)\cdot(\boldsymbol{v}-\boldsymbol{u})(\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{v})\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x}

\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma-\delta}{a_{1}\delta a}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\phi^{-2\iota}\big(\nabla\phi\cdot(\partial_{l}\boldsymbol{v}-\partial_{l}\boldsymbol{u})+\partial_{l}\phi(\diver\boldsymbol{v}-\diver\boldsymbol{u})\big)(\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{v})\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x}

\displaystyle+\frac{\gamma-1}{a_{1}\delta a}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\phi(\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{u})(\partial_{l}\diver\boldsymbol{v})\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x}.

Summing above over
l=1,\cdots\!,n
, we obtain from the spherical coordinate transformation that, for
a.e.

t\in(0,T)
,

(7.67)

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}+\frac{\gamma-1}{a_{1}\delta a}\Big|(r^{m}\phi)^{\frac{1}{2}-\iota}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}:=\sum_{i=15}^{19}G_{i},

where

\displaystyle G_{15}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\big(\frac{m}{r}u-u_{r}\big)\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r,

\displaystyle G_{16}

\displaystyle=-2\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\Big(u_{r}v_{r}+\frac{m}{r^{2}}uv\Big)_{r}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r,

\displaystyle G_{17}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\gamma-\delta}{a_{1}\delta a}\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\big(\frac{1-\delta}{\delta a}\phi^{-4\iota}\psi\phi_{r}+\phi^{-2\iota}\phi_{rr}\big)(v-u)\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r,

\displaystyle G_{18}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\gamma-\delta}{a_{1}\delta a}\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi^{-2\iota}\phi_{r}\Big(\big(2v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)-\big(2u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)\Big)\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r,

\displaystyle G_{19}

\displaystyle=\frac{\gamma-1}{a_{1}\delta a}\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\phi^{1-2\iota}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\,\mathrm{d}r.

For
G_{15}
–
G_{19}
, it follows from (
7.64
), Lemmas
4.9
,
5.5
,
7.7
–
7.9
,
7.11
,
A.1
, and
A.4
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} G_{15}&\leq C_{0}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}v|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}v|_{2}^{2},\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} G_{16}&\leq C_{0}\big(\big|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{m}{2}}|\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u||\mathrm{D}_{r}v|\big|_{2}+\big|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{\frac{m}{2}}|\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u||\mathrm{D}_{r}v|\big|_{2}+|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}v|_{2}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}v|_{2}\\
&\leq C_{0}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{3}u|_{2}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r\mathrm{D}_{r}v|_{\infty}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}\mathrm{D}_{r}v|_{\infty}+|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}v|_{2}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}v|_{2}\\
&\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}v|_{2}^{2}+1\big),\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} G_{17}&\leq C_{0}\big(|\phi|_{\infty}^{-4\iota}|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}+|\phi|_{\infty}^{-2\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{rr}|_{2}\big)|(u,v)|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}v|_{2}\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}v|_{2}^{2}+1\big),\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} G_{18}&\leq C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-2\iota}\big(|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}|(\chi_{1}^{\flat}rv_{r},v)|_{\infty}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}v_{r}|_{\infty}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}v|_{2}\\
&\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}v|_{2}^{2}+1\big),\end{aligned}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} G_{19}&\leq C|\phi|_{\infty}^{1-2\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}v|_{2}\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}v|_{2}^{2}+1\big).\end{aligned}

Thus, substituting the above into (
7.67
) leads to

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(v_{r}+\frac{m}{r}v\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}v|_{2}^{2}+1\big),

which, along with Lemmas
7.1
and
B.1
, and the Grönwall inequality, yields

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}v(t)|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}v_{0}|_{2}+1\big)\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{0},\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}\psi_{0})|_{2}+1\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\|(\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{u}_{0},\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{\psi}_{0})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+1\big)\leq C(T).

Certainly, by (
7.3
) and Lemma
7.7
, we also have

(7.68)

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}\psi(t)|_{2}\leq C_{0}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}v(t)|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u(t)|_{2}\big)\leq C(T).

3.
Applying
r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}
to
\eqref{e2.2}_{3}
and taking the
L^{2}(I)
-norm of the resulting equality, we obtain from (
7.1
), (
7.68
), and Lemmas
7.7
–
7.8
and
7.11
that

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}\psi_{t}|_{2}\leq C_{0}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{n^{*}}}u|_{n^{*}}|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\mathrm{D}_{r}\psi|_{n}+|(u,\psi)|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u,\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}\psi)|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{3}u|_{2}\big)\leq C(T).

This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 7.13
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that, for all
q\in[2\iota,0]
,

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}\phi_{r},\mathrm{D}_{r}\phi_{tr})(t)|_{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\big(|\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{\infty}^{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{q}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{4}u|_{2}^{2}\big)\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

We divide the proof into two steps.

1.
First, for the
L^{2}(I)
-estimate of
r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}\phi_{r}
, by Lemmas
4.9
,
7.7
–
7.9
, and
7.12
, we have

(7.69)

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}\phi_{r}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}(\phi^{1-2\iota}\psi)|_{2}\leq C_{0}\big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi^{-4\iota}\phi_{r}\psi^{2},\phi^{1-4\iota}\psi\mathrm{D}_{r}\psi,\phi^{1-2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}\psi)\big|_{2},

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-4\iota}\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}|_{2}|\psi|_{\infty}+|r^{\frac{m}{n^{*}}}\phi_{r}|_{n^{*}}|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\mathrm{D}_{r}\psi|_{n}\big)+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{1-2\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}\psi|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T).

Next, notice from (
7.58
) and Lemmas
4.9
,
7.7
–
7.9
, and
7.12
that

(7.70)

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}\phi_{tr}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}(\phi^{-2\iota}\phi_{t}\psi)|_{2}+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}(\phi^{1-2\iota}\psi_{t})|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi^{-4\iota}\phi_{t}\psi^{2},\phi^{-2\iota}\phi_{tr}\psi,\phi^{1-2\iota}(\log\phi)_{t}\mathrm{D}_{r}\psi,\phi^{-2\iota}\phi_{r}\psi_{t},\phi^{1-2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}\psi_{t})|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-4\iota}|\psi|_{\infty}^{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{t}|_{2}+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-2\iota}|(\phi,\psi)|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{tr},\mathrm{D}_{r}\psi_{t})|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-2\iota}|(\log\phi)_{t}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{n^{*}}}\phi|_{n^{*}}|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\mathrm{D}_{r}\psi|_{n}+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{-2\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}\psi_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}\psi_{t}|_{2}+1\big).

For the estimate of
|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}\psi_{t}|_{2}
on the right-hand side of (
7.70
), it follows from Lemmas
7.7
–
7.9
,
A.1
, and
A.4
that

(7.71)

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}\psi_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}\psi_{t}|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{r}\psi_{t}|_{2}\leq|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r\phi_{r}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\psi_{t}|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}\phi_{r}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{3}{2}}(\phi_{r},\phi_{rr})|_{2}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}(\phi_{r},\phi_{rr})|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{3-m}{2}}|_{\infty}+|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}r^{-\frac{m}{2}}|_{\infty}\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{r},\phi_{rr})|_{2}\leq C(T),

Combining with (
7.70
)–(
7.71
) yields

(7.72)

|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}\phi_{tr}(t)|_{2}\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for all $t\in[0,T]$}.

2.
Applying
r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}
to
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
and taking the
L^{2}(I)
-norm of the resulting equality, we first obtain
from (
7.69
), and Lemmas
7.7
–
7.8
and
7.11
–
7.12
that

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}\big(\phi^{2\iota}(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u)_{r}\big)\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t},|\mathrm{D}_{r}u||\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|,u\mathrm{D}_{r}^{3}u,\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}\phi_{r}\big)\big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}\big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(|\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}\psi||\mathrm{D}_{r}u|,|\mathrm{D}_{r}\psi||\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|,\psi\mathrm{D}_{r}^{3}u\big)\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t},\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}\phi_{r})|_{2}+C_{0}|(u,\psi)|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{3}u|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\mathrm{D}_{r}\psi|_{n}|r^{\frac{m}{n^{*}}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{n^{*}}+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u,\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}\psi)|_{2}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t}|_{2}+|\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{\infty}+1\big),

which also implies

(7.73)

\displaystyle\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}\big(\phi^{2\iota}(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u)_{r}\big)\Big|_{2}+C_{0}|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{3}u|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\mathrm{D}_{r}\psi|_{n}|r^{\frac{m}{n^{*}}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{n^{*}}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t}|_{2}+|\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{\infty}+1\big).

For the
L^{\infty}(I)
-estimate of
\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u
, we see Lemmas
4.9
,
7.1
,
7.7
,
A.2
and
B.1
that

(7.74)

\displaystyle|\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\|\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C_{0}\|\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{4-n}{4}}\|\nabla^{4}\boldsymbol{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{n}{4}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}^{\frac{4-n}{4}}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{4}u|_{2}^{\frac{n}{4}}\leq C(T)\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}\Big|_{2}^{\frac{n}{4}}.

Then (
7.73
), along with (
7.74
), Lemmas
7.2
,
7.7
, and
7.11
, and the Young inequality, gives

(7.75)

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{4}u|_{2}\leq C(T)\big(\big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u)_{r}\big|_{2}+1\big)\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t}|_{2}+1\big).

Finally, from (
7.74
) and Lemmas
4.9
and
7.11
, we obtain the desired estimates for
u
.
∎

7.6.
Time-Weighted Estimates of the Velocity

We now establish the time-weighted estimates for
u
.

Lemma 7.14
.

There exists a constant
C(T)>0
such that

\displaystyle t|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}(t)|^{2}_{2}+\int_{0}^{t}s|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{tt}|^{2}_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\qquad\text{for any $t\in[0,T]$}.

Proof.

We divide the proof into three steps.

1.

First, it follows from Lemmas
4.9
,
7.6
,
7.11
,
A.1
, and
A.4
that

(7.76)

\displaystyle|u_{t}|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|(u_{t},u_{tr})|_{2}\leq C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}(u_{t},u_{tr})|_{2}+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}(u_{t},u_{tr})|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r(u_{t},u_{tr},u_{trr})|_{2}+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}(u_{t},u_{tr})|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{t},u_{tr},u_{trr})|_{2}\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}u_{trr}|_{2}+1\big),

\displaystyle|(\chi_{1}^{\flat}r\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t},\chi_{1}^{\sharp}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t})|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r^{\frac{3}{2}}(\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t},\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t})|_{2}+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}(\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t},\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t})|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t},\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t})|_{2}\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t}|_{2}+1\big).

Next, applying
r^{\frac{m}{2}}\partial_{t}
to
\eqref{e2.2}_{3}
and taking the
L^{2}(I)
-norm of both sides of the resulting equality, we obtain from (
7.58
), (
7.76
), and
Lemmas
7.7
–
7.8
and
7.10
–
7.12
that

(7.77)

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\big(u_{t}\psi_{r},u\psi_{tr},\psi_{t}\mathrm{D}_{r}u,\psi\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t},\phi^{2\iota}(\log\phi)_{t}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u,\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t}\big)\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|(u,\psi)|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\mathrm{D}_{r}u,\psi_{tr})|_{2}+|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{t}|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{n^{*}}}u_{t}|_{n^{*}}|r^{\frac{m}{n}}\psi_{r}|_{n}\big)

\displaystyle+C_{0}|(\log\phi)_{t}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t}|_{2}+1\big).

Finally, based on
\eqref{e2.2}_{1}
, it follows from Lemmas
4.9
,
7.6
–
7.9
,
and
7.11
that

(7.78)

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u\phi_{r})_{t}|_{2}+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi\mathrm{D}_{r}u)_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{tr}|_{2}+|\phi|_{\infty}|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{t}|_{2}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{1-\iota}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{2}\leq C(T),

which, along with the chain rule, (
7.1
), (
7.58
), (
7.77
), and Lemma
7.7
, leads to

(7.79)

\displaystyle|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{ttr}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi^{-2\iota}\phi_{tt}\psi,\phi^{-2\iota}(\log\phi)_{t}\phi_{t}\psi,\phi^{1-2\iota}(\log\phi)_{t}\psi_{t},\phi^{1-2\iota}\psi_{tt})\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\big(|\phi|_{\infty}^{-2\iota}|(\phi,\psi)|_{\infty}\big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(\phi_{tt},\psi_{tt})\big|_{2}+|\phi|_{\infty}^{-2\iota}|(\log\phi)_{t}|_{\infty}|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi_{t}|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle+C_{0}|\phi|_{\infty}^{1-2\iota}|(\log\phi)_{t}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{t}|_{2}\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t}|_{2}+1\big).

2.
We now proceed to prove Lemma
7.14
.
Formally, applying
r^{m}u_{tt}\partial_{t}^{2}
to both sides of
\eqref{e2.2}_{2}
and integrating the resulting equality over
I
yield

(7.80)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}^{2}+2a_{1}\delta a\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{ttr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{tt}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=4a_{1}\delta a\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}(\phi^{2\iota})_{t}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)_{r}u_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}r+2a_{1}\delta a\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}(\phi^{2\iota})_{tt}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)_{r}u_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle+2a_{1}\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\Big(\psi_{tt}\big(\delta u_{r}+m(\delta-1)\frac{u}{r}\big)+2\psi_{t}\big(\delta u_{tr}+m(\delta-1)\frac{u_{t}}{r}\big)+\psi u_{ttr}\Big)u_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{m}\big((uu_{r})_{tt}+\phi_{ttr}\big)u_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}r:=\sum_{i=20}^{23}G_{i}

for
a.e.

t\in(\tau,T)
and
\tau\in(0,T)
. Here, we temporarily assume that energy equality (
7.80
) holds, and the specific proof will be given in Step 3 below.

Then, for
G_{20}
, it follows from (
7.58
), (
7.76
),
Lemmas
4.9
,
7.1
,
7.8
, and
7.12
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(7.81)

\displaystyle G_{20}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|(\log\phi)_{t}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\psi_{t}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{t}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}+C_{0}|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{ttr}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t}|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{ttr}|_{2}+1\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}^{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+1\big)+\frac{a_{1}}{8}\Big|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{\iota}\big(u_{ttr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{tt}\big)\Big|_{2}^{2}.

To estimate
G_{21}
, we first see from (
7.1
) and (
7.27
) that

(\phi^{2\iota})_{tt}=(1-\delta)\Big(\frac{1}{\delta a}(u_{t}\psi+u\psi_{t})+2\iota\phi^{2\iota}(\log\phi)_{t}\big(u_{r}+\frac{m}{r}u\big)+\phi^{2\iota}\big(u_{tr}+\frac{m}{r}u_{t}\big)\Big).

Then it follows from the above, (
7.58
), (
7.76
),
Lemmas
7.7
–
7.8
and
7.11
–
7.12
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(7.82)

\displaystyle G_{21}

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}|(u,\psi)|_{\infty}|\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}(u_{t},\psi_{t})|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|(\log\phi)_{t}|_{\infty}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{\infty}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t}|_{2}+1\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}^{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+|\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u|_{\infty}^{2}+1\big).

For
G_{22}
–
G_{23}
, it follows from
\eqref{e2.2}_{3}
, (
7.76
)–(
7.77
), (
7.79
), Lemmas
4.9
,
7.1
,
7.7
–
7.8
, and
7.11
–
7.12
, and the Hölder and Young inequalities that

(7.83)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} G_{22}&\leq C_{0}|\mathrm{D}_{r}u|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{tt}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\flat}r\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m-2}{2}}\psi_{t}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}\\
&\quad+C_{0}|\chi_{1}^{\sharp}\mathrm{D}_{r}u_{t}|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\psi_{t}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}+C_{0}|\psi|_{\infty}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{ttr}|_{2}|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}\\
&\leq C(T)\big(|r^{\frac{m}{2}}\phi^{2\iota}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{2}u_{t}|_{2}+|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{ttr}|_{2}+1\big)|r^{\frac{m}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}\\
