\displaystyle=\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\Big(\frac{\beta-\alpha}{\alpha}\frac{\beta-2\alpha}{\alpha}f^{\beta-3\alpha}(f^{\alpha})_{x_{i}}(f^{\alpha})_{x_{j}}(f^{\alpha})_{x_{k}}+\frac{\beta-\alpha}{\alpha}f^{\beta-2\alpha}(f^{\alpha})_{x_{i}x_{k}}(f^{\alpha})_{x_{j}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\frac{\beta-\alpha}{\alpha}f^{\beta-2\alpha}\Big((f^{\alpha})_{x_{i}}(f^{\alpha})_{x_{j}x_{k}}+(f^{\alpha})_{x_{k}}(f^{\alpha})_{x_{i}x_{j}}\big)+\frac{\beta}{\alpha}f^{\beta-\alpha}(f^{\alpha})_{x_{i}x_{j}x_{k}}\Big).

Then it follows from
\beta\in[\alpha+\frac{1}{2},\infty)
, (
A.8
)–(
A.9
), and the Hölder inequality that

\displaystyle\|\nabla f^{\beta}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|f\|^{\beta-\alpha}_{L^{2\beta-2\alpha}}\|\nabla f^{\alpha}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C,

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{2}(f^{\beta})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|f\|_{L^{2\beta-4\alpha}}^{\beta-2\alpha}\|\nabla f^{\alpha}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+C\|f\|_{L^{n^{*}(\beta-\alpha)}}^{\beta-\alpha}\|\nabla^{2}f^{\alpha}\|_{L^{n}}\leq C,

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{3}(f^{\beta})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|f\|_{L^{2\beta-6\alpha}}^{\beta-3\alpha}\|\nabla f^{\alpha}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{3}+C\|f\|_{L^{\infty}}^{\beta-\alpha}\|\nabla^{3}f^{\alpha}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+C\|f\|_{L^{n^{*}(\beta-2\alpha)}}^{\beta-2\alpha}\|\nabla f^{\alpha}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{2}f^{\alpha}\|_{L^{n}}\leq C.

This completes the proof.
∎

Next, to establish the
L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
-estimates for the transport equation:

(A.10)

g_{t}+\diver(\boldsymbol{w}g)=f,

we need the following lemma to justify the energy equality.

Lemma A.11

(
[
13
,
40
]
)
.

Let
n\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
and
T>0
. Assume that

f\in L^{1}([0,T];L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{n})),\quad\boldsymbol{w}\in L^{1}([0,T];W^{1,\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{n})),\quad g\in L^{\infty}([0,T];L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{n})),

for some
p\in[2,\infty)
, satisfying (
A.10
) in the sense of distributions. Then

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|g\|^{p}_{L^{p}}=-(p-1)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}|g|^{p}\diver\boldsymbol{w}\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x}+p\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}|g|^{p-2}gf\,\mathrm{d}\boldsymbol{x}\qquad\text{for {\it a.e.} $t\in(0,T)$}.

Finally, we need to the following estimates on the equation for the Lamé operator
L
:

(A.11)

L\boldsymbol{f}=-a_{1}\Delta\boldsymbol{f}-(a_{1}+a_{2})\nabla\diver\boldsymbol{f}=\boldsymbol{g}\qquad\text{in $\mathbb{R}^{n}$}.

Lemma A.12

(
[
54
]
)
.

Let
q\in(1,\infty)
, and let
\boldsymbol{f}\in D^{1,q}(\mathbb{R}^{n})

be a weak solution to (
A.11
) with the asymptotic condition
\boldsymbol{f}\to\boldsymbol{0}
as
|\boldsymbol{x}|\to\infty
.
Then, if
\boldsymbol{g}\in L^{q}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
, there exists a constant
C>0
, depending only on
(n,a_{1},k,q)
and independent of
(\boldsymbol{f},\boldsymbol{g})
, such that

\|\nabla^{2}\boldsymbol{f}\|_{L^{q}(\mathbb{R}^{n})}\leq C\|\boldsymbol{g}\|_{L^{q}(\mathbb{R}^{n})}.

Appendix B
Conversion of Sobolev Spaces for Spherically Symmetric Functions

This appendix is devoted to showing the conversion of some Sobolev spaces between the M-D Eulerian coordinate
\boldsymbol{x}
and the spherical coordinate
r
for spherically symmetric functions.
Let
n
be the spatial dimensions and
m=n-1
.

Lemma B.1

(
[
13
]
)
.

Let
q\in[1,\infty]
,
0\leq a<b\leq\infty
,
\Omega:=\{\boldsymbol{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{n}:\,a\leq|\boldsymbol{x}|<b\}
, and
r\in J:=[a,b)
with
r=|\boldsymbol{x}|
.
Then

\mathrm{(i)}

For spherically symmetric function
f\in W^{3,q}(\Omega)
with
f(\boldsymbol{x})=f(r)
,

\|f\|_{L^{q}(\Omega)}\sim\|r^{\frac{m}{q}}f\|_{L^{q}(J)},\qquad\|\nabla^{j}f\|_{L^{q}(\Omega)}\sim\|r^{\frac{m}{q}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{j-1}f_{r}\|_{L^{q}(J)}\quad\text{for $j=1,2,3$};

\mathrm{(ii)}

For spherically symmetric vector function
\boldsymbol{f}\in W^{4,q}(\Omega)
with
\boldsymbol{f}(\boldsymbol{x})=f(r)\frac{\boldsymbol{x}}{r}
,

\|\nabla^{j}\boldsymbol{f}\|_{L^{q}(\Omega)}\sim\|r^{\frac{m}{q}}\mathrm{D}_{r}^{j}f\|_{L^{q}(J)}\quad\text{for $j=0,1,2,3,4$}.

Here,
E\sim F
denotes
C^{-1}E\leq F\leq CE
for some constant
C\geq 1
depending only on
n
.

Appendix C
Sobolev Embeddings for Spherically Symmetric Functions

In this appendix, we give several improved Sobolev embeddings of the type:
D^{1,p}(\mathbb{R}^{n})\hookrightarrow L^{q}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
for spherically symmetric vector functions when
p\in[1,n]
. The proofs of the following two lemmas can be found in
[
13
, Appendix C]
.

Lemma C.1

(
[
13
]
)
.

Let
\boldsymbol{f}(\boldsymbol{x})=f(r)\frac{\boldsymbol{x}}{r}
be any spherically symmetric vector function defined in
\mathbb{R}^{n}

(
n\geq 2
)
. If
\boldsymbol{f}\in D^{1,p}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
for some
p\in[1,n)
, then
\boldsymbol{f}\in L^{\frac{np}{n-p}}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
, and there exists a constant
C(n,p)>0
depending only on
(n,p)
such that

(C.1)

\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{L^{\frac{np}{n-p}}(\mathbb{R}^{n})}\leq C(n,p)\|\nabla\boldsymbol{f}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{n})}.

Lemma C.2

(
[
13
]
)
.

Let
\boldsymbol{f}(\boldsymbol{x})=f(r)\frac{\boldsymbol{x}}{r}
be any spherically symmetric vector function defined in
\mathbb{R}^{n}

(n\geq 2)
. If
\boldsymbol{f}\in D^{1,n}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
, then
\boldsymbol{f}\in C(\overline{\mathbb{R}^{n}})
, and there exists a uniform constant
C(n)>0
depending only on
n
such that

(C.2)

\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{n})}\leq C(n)\|\nabla\boldsymbol{f}\|_{L^{n}(\mathbb{R}^{n})}.

Remark C.1
.

Lemmas
C.1
–
C.2
do not hold for general vector functions or scalar functions.

For
Lemma
C.1
, such examples include

\boldsymbol{f}=(1,\cdots\!,1)^{\top}
or
f=1
. This is mainly owing to the fact that any spherically symmetric constant vector function must vanish.

For
Lemma
C.2
, consider a function
f=f(z)
defined on
[0,\infty)
satisfying

f(0)=0,\qquad f(z)=|\log z|^{\frac{1}{3}}\quad\text{on $(0,e^{-1}]$},\qquad f(z)=(ez)^{-2}\quad\text{on $(e^{-1},\infty)$}.

Define
g(\boldsymbol{x})\!=\!f(|\boldsymbol{x}|)
and
\boldsymbol{h}(\boldsymbol{x})
with
h_{i}(\boldsymbol{x})=f(|x_{i}|)

(1\leq i\leq n)
.
It can be checked that
(g,\boldsymbol{h})
admits the weak derivatives
(\partial_{j}g,\partial_{j}\boldsymbol{h})\in L^{n}(\mathbb{R}^{n})

(1\leq j\leq n)
, while
(g,\boldsymbol{h})\notin L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{n})
.

Appendix D
Some Auxiliary Calculations

This section is devoted to providing the detailed calculations of (
6.3
) and (
6.15
). To this end, we only need to show the following lemma.

Lemma D.1
.

Let
\ell=2,3,4,5
. Then the following equality holds
:

\displaystyle\,\Big(\rho u_{t}+\rho uu_{r}-2a_{1}\delta\big(\rho^{\delta}u_{r}+\frac{m\rho^{\delta}u}{r}\big)_{r}+2a_{1}m(\rho^{\delta})_{r}\frac{u}{r}\Big)\times(\rho^{-\alpha}|u|^{\ell-2}u)

\displaystyle=\big(\frac{1}{\ell}\rho^{1-\alpha}|u|^{\ell}\big)_{t}+2(\ell-1)a_{1}\delta\rho^{\delta-\alpha}|u|^{\ell-2}\Big(u_{r}^{2}-m\frac{1-\delta}{\delta-\alpha}u_{r}\frac{u}{r}+\frac{m}{\ell}\frac{1-\alpha}{\delta-\alpha}\frac{u^{2}}{r^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad-\Big(a_{1}\delta\rho^{\delta-\alpha}|u|^{\ell-2}\big(2uu_{r}+2m\frac{\ell\delta-\ell\alpha-\ell+1}{\ell(\delta-\alpha)}\frac{u^{2}}{r}\big)-\frac{(\ell-1)\alpha+\ell+1}{\ell(\ell+1)}\rho^{1-\alpha}u|u|^{\ell}\Big)_{r}

\displaystyle\quad-\rho^{1-\alpha}vu|u|^{\ell-2}\Big(\alpha u_{r}-\frac{m(1-\alpha)}{\ell}\frac{u}{r}\Big)-\frac{(\ell-1)\alpha(1-\alpha)}{2\ell(\ell+1)a_{1}\delta}\rho^{2-\delta-\alpha}(v-u)u|u|^{\ell}.

Proof.

First, it follows from (
4.9
) and (
6.2
) that

\displaystyle\,(\rho u_{t}+\rho uu_{r})\times(\rho^{-\alpha}|u|^{\ell-2}u)

\displaystyle=\big(\frac{1}{\ell}\rho^{1-\alpha}|u|^{\ell}\big)_{t}+\Big(\frac{1}{\ell}\rho^{1-\alpha}|u|^{\ell}u\Big)_{r}-\frac{1}{\ell}|u|^{\ell}\big((\rho^{1-\alpha})_{t}+(\rho^{1-\alpha}u)_{r}\big)

\displaystyle=\big(\frac{1}{\ell}\rho^{1-\alpha}|u|^{\ell}\big)_{t}+\Big(\frac{1}{\ell}\rho^{1-\alpha}|u|^{\ell}u\Big)_{r}-\frac{\alpha}{\ell}\rho^{1-\alpha}|u|^{\ell}u_{r}+\frac{m(1-\alpha)}{\ell}\frac{\rho^{1-\alpha}|u|^{\ell}u}{r}

\displaystyle=\big(\frac{1}{\ell}\rho^{1-\alpha}|u|^{\ell}\big)_{t}+\Big(\frac{1}{\ell}\rho^{1-\alpha}|u|^{\ell}u\Big)_{r}-\frac{\alpha}{\ell(\ell+1)}\rho^{1-\alpha}(|u|^{\ell}u)_{r}+\frac{m(1-\alpha)}{\ell}\frac{\rho^{1-\alpha}|u|^{\ell}u}{r}

\displaystyle=\big(\frac{1}{\ell}\rho^{1-\alpha}|u|^{\ell}\big)_{t}+\Big(\frac{\ell+1-\alpha}{\ell(\ell+1)}\rho^{1-\alpha}|u|^{\ell}u\Big)_{r}+\frac{\alpha(1-\alpha)}{\ell(\ell+1)}\rho^{-\alpha}\rho_{r}|u|^{\ell}u+\frac{m(1-\alpha)}{\ell}\frac{\rho^{1-\alpha}|u|^{\ell}u}{r}

\displaystyle=\big(\frac{1}{\ell}\rho^{1-\alpha}|u|^{\ell}\big)_{t}+\Big(\frac{\ell+1-\alpha}{\ell(\ell+1)}\rho^{1-\alpha}|u|^{\ell}u\Big)_{r}+\frac{\alpha(1-\alpha)}{2\ell(\ell+1)a_{1}\delta}\rho^{2-\delta-\alpha}(v-u)u|u|^{\ell}

\displaystyle\quad+\boxed{\frac{m(1-\alpha)}{\ell}\frac{\rho^{1-\alpha}|u|^{\ell}u}{r}}.

Next, using (
4.9
) again, we have

\displaystyle\,\Big(2a_{1}\delta\big(\rho^{\delta}u_{r}+\frac{m\rho^{\delta}u}{r}\big)_{r}-2a_{1}m(\rho^{\delta})_{r}\frac{u}{r}\Big)\times(\rho^{-\alpha}|u|^{\ell-2}u)

\displaystyle=\Big(2a_{1}\delta\rho^{\delta-\alpha}|u|^{\ell-2}\big(uu_{r}+m\frac{u^{2}}{r}\big)\Big)_{r}-2a_{1}\delta\rho^{\delta}\big(u_{r}+m\frac{u}{r}\big)(\rho^{-\alpha}|u|^{\ell-2}u)_{r}-2a_{1}\delta m\rho^{\delta-\alpha-1}\rho_{r}\frac{|u|^{\ell}}{r}

\displaystyle=\Big(2a_{1}\delta\rho^{\delta-\alpha}|u|^{\ell-2}\big(uu_{r}+m\frac{u^{2}}{r}\big)\Big)_{r}-2(\ell-1)a_{1}\delta\rho^{\delta-\alpha}|u|^{\ell-2}\big(u_{r}^{2}+mu_{r}\frac{u}{r}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+2a_{1}\delta\alpha\rho^{\delta-\alpha-1}\rho_{r}|u|^{\ell-2}uu_{r}-2a_{1}\delta m(1-\alpha)\rho^{\delta-\alpha-1}\rho_{r}\frac{|u|^{\ell}}{r}

\displaystyle=\Big(2a_{1}\delta\rho^{\delta-\alpha}|u|^{\ell-2}\big(uu_{r}+m\frac{u^{2}}{r}\big)\Big)_{r}-2(\ell-1)a_{1}\delta\rho^{\delta-\alpha}|u|^{\ell-2}\big(u_{r}^{2}+mu_{r}\frac{u}{r}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+\alpha\rho^{1-\alpha}v|u|^{\ell-2}uu_{r}-\alpha\rho^{1-\alpha}|u|^{\ell}u_{r}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{2(\ell-1)}{\ell}a_{1}\delta m\frac{1-\alpha}{\delta-\alpha}(\rho^{\delta-\alpha})_{r}\frac{|u|^{\ell}}{r}-\frac{2}{\ell}a_{1}\delta m(1-\alpha)\rho^{\delta-\alpha-1}\rho_{r}\frac{|u|^{\ell}}{r}

\displaystyle=\Big(2a_{1}\delta\rho^{\delta-\alpha}|u|^{\ell-2}\big(uu_{r}+m\frac{u^{2}}{r}\big)\Big)_{r}-2(\ell-1)a_{1}\delta\rho^{\delta-\alpha}|u|^{\ell-2}\big(u_{r}^{2}+mu_{r}\frac{u}{r}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+\alpha\rho^{1-\alpha}v|u|^{\ell-2}uu_{r}-\frac{\alpha}{\ell+1}\rho^{1-\alpha}(|u|^{\ell}u)_{r}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{2(\ell-1)}{\ell}a_{1}\delta m\frac{1-\alpha}{\delta-\alpha}\Big(\rho^{\delta-\alpha}\frac{|u|^{\ell}}{r}\Big)_{r}+\frac{2(\ell-1)}{\ell}a_{1}\delta m\frac{1-\alpha}{\delta-\alpha}\rho^{\delta-\alpha}\big(\frac{|u|^{\ell}}{r}\big)_{r}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{m(1-\alpha)}{\ell}\frac{\rho^{1-\alpha}v|u|^{\ell}}{r}+\frac{m(1-\alpha)}{\ell}\frac{\rho^{1-\alpha}u|u|^{\ell}}{r}

\displaystyle=\Big(a_{1}\delta\rho^{\delta-\alpha}|u|^{\ell-2}\big(2uu_{r}+2m\frac{\ell\delta-\alpha-(\ell-1)}{\ell(\delta-\alpha)}\frac{u^{2}}{r}\big)-\frac{\alpha}{\ell+1}\rho^{1-\alpha}|u|^{\ell}u\Big)_{r}

\displaystyle\quad-2(\ell-1)a_{1}\delta\rho^{\delta-\alpha}|u|^{\ell-2}\big(u_{r}^{2}+mu_{r}\frac{u}{r}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+\rho^{1-\alpha}vu|u|^{\ell-2}\Big(\alpha u_{r}-\frac{m(1-\alpha)}{\ell}\frac{u}{r}\Big)+\frac{m(1-\alpha)}{\ell}\frac{\rho^{1-\alpha}|u|^{\ell}u}{r}+\frac{\alpha(1-\alpha)}{\ell+1}\rho^{-\alpha}\rho_{r}|u|^{\ell}u

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2(\ell-1)}{\ell}a_{1}\delta m\frac{1-\alpha}{\delta-\alpha}\rho^{\delta-\alpha}|u|^{\ell-2}\big(\ell u_{r}\frac{u}{r}-\frac{u^{2}}{r^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle=\Big(a_{1}\delta\rho^{\delta-\alpha}|u|^{\ell-2}\big(2uu_{r}+2m\frac{\ell\delta-\alpha-(\ell-1)}{\ell(\delta-\alpha)}\frac{u^{2}}{r}\big)-\frac{\alpha}{\ell+1}\rho^{1-\alpha}|u|^{\ell}u\Big)_{r}

\displaystyle\quad-2(\ell-1)a_{1}\delta\rho^{\delta-\alpha}|u|^{\ell-2}\Big(u_{r}^{2}-m\frac{1-\delta}{\delta-\alpha}u_{r}\frac{u}{r}+\frac{m}{\ell}\frac{1-\alpha}{\delta-\alpha}\frac{u^{2}}{r^{2}}\Big)+\boxed{\frac{m(1-\alpha)}{\ell}\frac{\rho^{1-\alpha}|u|^{\ell}u}{r}}

\displaystyle\quad+\rho^{1-\alpha}vu|u|^{\ell-2}\Big(\alpha u_{r}-\frac{m(1-\alpha)}{\ell}\frac{u}{r}\Big)+\frac{\alpha(1-\alpha)}{2(\ell+1)a_{1}\delta}\rho^{2-\delta-\alpha}(v-u)u|u|^{\ell}.

Therefore, combining the above two equalities, we arrive at the desired equality by canceling the above two framed terms.
∎
